Method: 构建 ReTurn 基准，含 7,000 个基础任务，覆盖视觉与音频证据。针对"任务承载历史"设置 Reconfirm/Reground（将历史问题应用于当前媒介并改变历史一致性）；针对"证据承载历史"设置 Retrieve/Rebind（用历史媒介回答当前问题并改变当前媒介竞争）。每组保持目标问题、媒介与答案不变，支持开放式与多选评估，并配匹单轮对照作为可答性参照。

Result: 在 13 个全模态、视觉语言与音频语言模型上，中位数模型级开放式准确率从直接输入的 93.7% 降至对话中的 72.3%。行为探针显示高问题回忆可与较弱任务应用并存，竞争媒介会将答案从历史目标上转移。监督适配仅带来部分提升。

Conclusion: ReTurn 提供了受控框架，用于评估多模态模型是否选择并使用了每个请求所需的历史信息。

Abstract: Reliable multimodal interaction depends on selective use of conversational history: an earlier question may remain relevant while its previous answer is outdated, whereas a current request may depend on historical evidence despite conflicting new observations. Existing multi-turn evaluations rarely separate these history-use demands from underlying question difficulty. To address this gap, we introduce ReTurn, a benchmark of 7,000 base tasks spanning visual and audio evidence for evaluating selective history use. For task-carrying history, Reconfirm/Reground require applying a historical question to current media while varying historical agreement; for evidence-carrying history, Retrieve/Rebind require answering a current question using historical media while varying current-media competition. Each pair preserves the target question, media, and answer. Tasks support open-ended and multiple-choice evaluation, with matched single-turn counterparts serving as answerability references. Across 13 omni-modal, vision-language, and audio-language models, median model-level open-ended accuracy falls from 93.7% with direct input to 72.3% in conversation. Behavioral probes show that high question recall can coexist with weaker task application, while competing media can redirect answers away from historical targets. Supervised adaptation yields only partial gains. ReTurn provides a controlled framework for assessing whether multimodal models select and use the historical information required by each request.

</details>


### [71] [MiMo-V2.6: Scaling Reinforcement Learning Towards Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11959)
*Xiaomi LLM-Core Team,:,Zongming Qiao,Ziyue Hua,Zirui Ou,Zihao Yue,Zihan Jiang,Zhuo Huang,Zhiyang Chen,Zhixian Zheng,Zhipeng Xu,Zhengrui Ma,Yuyang Hu,Yuhang Dong,Yuechen Zhang,Yudong Wang,Yuanxin Liu,Yixin Yang,Yishuo Cai,Yikai Zhao,Yihan Yan,Yifan Zhang,Yifan Song,Xiyu Wei,Xing Zhang,Xin Zhang,Xiaoqian Liu,Xiaodong Ji,Xiangwei Deng,Xueyu Guo,Wenhan Ma,Weimin Xiong,Weikun Wang,Weiji Zhuang,Shuo Liu,Shuhuai Ren,Shuhao Gu,Shimao Chen,Shijie Cao,Shihua Yu,Shicheng Li,Shengjie Zhou,Shaolei Zhang,Rang Li,Qiying Wang,Qingkai Fang,Qianli Chen,Minzheng Wang,Liwen Wang,Linli Yao,Linghao Zhang,Liangyu Cheng,Liang Zhao,Lei Li,Jinhao Dong,Jinyu Xiang,Jianyu Wei,Jiangshan Duo,Huaqiu Liu,Huanjie Fan,Hongyi Guan,Hongshen Xu,Hao Tian,Hanyu Li,Hailin Zhang,Gang Wang,Fuli Luo,Feng Wei,Dong Zhang,Dawei Zhu,Chiheng Lou,Chenhong He,Chenhao He,Chenghua Liu,Bowen Ye,Bowen Shen,Boshen Xu,Bo Yang,Bingquan Xia,Bangjun Xiao,Baixuan Xu,Zhouxiang Mao,Zhiyang Zhang,Zhixiang Xu,Zhenru Lin,Zhengju Tang,Zhaojun Huang,Yuzhe Weng,Yuxing Xiang,Yuxiao Li,Yuheng Yang,Yuhang Wang,Yuchen Liu,Yuanyuan Tian,Yuanliang Dong,Yu Cheng,Yongzhe He,Yongshun Liang,Yong Wang,Yiyan Wang,Yitian Gong,Yijie Zhang,Yanshu Xin,Xun Zhang,Xingjian Zhao,Wenyu Yang,Wenshan Huang,Wenhao Li,Tingwei Huang,Tianyu Yu,Tianyang Lu,Taoyu Yang,Sinan Du,Shutong Tian,Shulin Du,Shengfan Wang,Shanchuan Fang,Qihao Zhang,Qibin Yang,Qian Yu,Qian Tu,Pengrong Xie,Peipei Wang,Peidian Li,Minkun Guo,Mingchen Shao,Luohan Gao,Lijie Wang,Liang Shi,Kaiqi Chen,Kaiming Liu,Kaifei Wang,Kai Yang,Jinlong Xue,Jiechen Zhang,Jiaxuan Liu,Hongxu An,Hao Peng,Hanglong Lü,Guonan Wang,Feiyu Yang,Fanyu Cao,Fangyue Liu,Fan Cui,Cong Wang,Chun Chen,Chenxu Bai,Chengxuan Zhu,Chenghua Wang,Boyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍 MiMo-V2.6 系列全模态模型，通过从三个维度扩展强化学习（RL）算力来推动模型智能前沿，并开源训练动态、RL 环境与框架以促进可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习是推动大型基础模型走向自我提升的核心训练范式。为进一步提升模型智能水平，需要系统性地扩大 RL 算力规模，同时解决大规模训练中的稳定性与奖励信号准确性问题。

Method: RL 之前，在大规模多模态语料上开展 mid-training 以提供充足探索空间，并在预训练的 hybrid-SWA 架构上构建基础设施。RL 算力沿三维度扩展：(1) 更大批量与更高吞吐，采用异步训练，每步消耗 1,568 个样本、2.7–3.7B tokens，上下文长度最高达 1M；(2) 更丰富复杂的环境，覆盖代码、通用、视觉与网络领域，混合多种 agent harness；(3) 更多评分算力，采用 groupwise agentic grading 为长时程任务提供更准确奖励信号，并引导模型生成更短、token 更高效的解。为保持稳定性，冻结 MoE router 并建立多层防御以对抗 reward hacking。此外构建混合任务 agentic RL 基础设施，包括统一轨迹表示、高并发多框架 rollout、控制面与数据面解耦，以及训练-推理一致性。

Result: 实现了全模态 MiMo-V2.6 系列模型，在扩展 RL 算力的过程中保持了大规模训练的稳定性，提升了奖励信号准确性，并使模型倾向于更短、更 token 高效的长时程任务解。

Conclusion: 通过多维度扩展 RL 算力并结合稳定性与反奖励攻击机制，可有效提升全模态基础模型智能；相关训练动态、RL 环境与 RL 框架已开源，以促进规模化 RL 与模型自我提升的研究与复现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is the central training paradigm for advancing large foundation models towards self-improvement. This report introduces the MiMo-V2.6 series, an omni-modal family that pushes the frontier of model intelligence by scaling RL compute. Prior to RL, we conduct mid-training on a broad multimodal corpus to provide ample exploration space, and build a solid infrastructure on the pretrained hybrid-SWA architecture to support subsequent scale-up. We scale RL compute along three dimensions: (1) larger batches and higher throughput, with an asynchronous training that consumes 1,568 samples and 2.7-3.7B tokens per step at context lengths of up to 1M; (2) more diverse and complex environments, spanning code, general, visual, and cyber domains under a mixture of agent harnesses; and (3) more grader compute, via groupwise agentic grading that yields more accurate reward signals for long-horizon tasks and steers the model towards shorter, more token-efficient solutions. To keep training stable at scale, we freeze the MoE router and establish a multi-layer defense against reward hacking. We further build infrastructure for mixed-task agentic RL, including a unified trajectory representation, high-concurrency multi-framework rollout, decoupled control and data planes, and training-inference consistency. We open-source the training dynamics, RL environments, and RL framework to facilitate reproduction and further research on scaled RL and model self-improvement.

</details>


### [72] [Specialized Decision Models vs. General-Purpose LLMs: Benchmarking Jev Across Knowledge, Reasoning, and Multilingual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11978)
*Xing Li,Qingcheng Chang,Jinzhong Ning,Changfeng Xu,Shenlong Zhang,Yijia Zhang,Ling Luo,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了专用决策模型Jev与19个通用大语言模型在13个多选题基准上的表现：Jev在知识与常识任务上达到前沿水平，但在数学应用题上明显落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev是返回选项而非生成文本的"系统一"专用决策模型，作者希望探究这种专用决策模型与通用大语言模型相比的表现差异。

Method: 在覆盖知识、推理与多语言理解的13个多选题基准上评估Jev，并将其与分三档（前沿、代表性、小型）的19个LLM进行对比。

Result: Jev在知识与常识基准上与前沿LLM相当，在MMLU-Redux和ARC-Challenge上取得最高分；除数学外，优于多数代表性LLM和所有小型LLM；但在数学应用题上落后，MathQA上比前沿中位数低17.7分，且低于全部19个LLM。

Conclusion: 专用决策模型能在主要依赖知识的决策任务上媲美通用LLM，但在需要多步计算的任务上无法匹敌。

Abstract: Jev is a "System One" model that returns a choice among given options instead of generating text. We study how such a specialized decision model compares with general-purpose large language models (LLMs). We evaluate Jev on 13 multiple-choice benchmarks covering knowledge, reasoning, and multilingual understanding, and compare it with 19 LLMs in three tiers: frontier, representative, and small. Jev is competitive with frontier LLMs on knowledge and commonsense benchmarks and obtains the best score on MMLU-Redux and ARC-Challenge. Outside mathematics, it also outperforms most representative LLMs and all small LLMs. However, it falls behind on mathematical word problems: on MathQA, it is 17.7 points below the frontier median and scores lower than all 19 LLMs. These results indicate that a specialized decision model can match general-purpose LLMs on decisions that rely mainly on knowledge, but not on decisions that require multi-step calculation.

</details>


### [73] [Examining Social Attribution in LLM Reasoning: A Theory-Guided Probing Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12022)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qingchao Kong,Dajun Zeng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统探究大语言模型（LLM）的社会归因能力，聚焦责任与责备判断，构建7,639题基准并评估32个LLM，结合探针方法分析其潜在表征机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益嵌入社会技术系统，社会归因（将外部事件归因于行为主体的原因与理由）至关重要；归因理论在心理学中已成熟，但在AI尤其LLM社会推理中研究不足。

Method: 以归因理论为指导，构建社会归因基准（经典情境Vignette子集+真实社会叙事Reality子集，共7,639个责任/责备判断题）；评估32个代表性LLM及5个非LLM基线；提出基于探针的方法，考察5个关键归因维度在隐空间的表征及其对人类归因理论影响一致性。

Result: 当前LLM与人类责任/责备判断存在可测量但不完全一致，一致性随模型规模增大而提升；部分归因维度可从LLM隐状态特定位置系统解码，其对最终判断的影响与人类归因理论一致。

Conclusion: LLM具备一定社会归因能力但尚不完善，其内部机制部分体现类人归因规律；数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in sociotechnical systems where social attribution, the reasoning process attributing external events to the causes and reasons of agents' social behaviors, plays a critical role. These processes involve judgments of social cause, responsibility, and blame/credit to agents. Although attributional models are well-studied in social psychology and cognition through Attribution Theory, social attribution remains underexplored in AI, particularly LLM social reasoning. This paper provides the first systematic exploration of LLM social attribution. Our work focuses on responsibility and blame attributions, examining current LLMs' judgments and their underlying internal mechanisms. Guided by attribution theory, we construct a social attribution benchmark consisting of a Vignette subset based on classic scenarios from attribution theory research and a Reality subset based on real-world social narratives, yielding 7,639 responsibility/blame judgment questions. On this basis, we evaluate 32 representative LLMs and 5 basic non-LLM baselines. To further explore the internal mechanisms underlying the LLM judgment process, we develop a probing-based methodology to investigate the latent-space representations of 5 key attribution dimensions and the consistency of their influences on LLM judgments compared to those in human social attribution. Our research findings reveal that current LLMs exhibit measurable but incomplete agreement with human responsibility and blame judgments, and meanwhile, this agreement is positively correlated with model size. Some attribution dimensions are systematically decodable from specific positions in LLM hidden states, and their influences on the final judgment are consistent with those indicated by human Attribution Theory. The dataset and associated code are available at https://github.com/Yuzhaoxin946/SAB-Bench.

</details>


### [74] [Natural Language to First-Order Logic LLM-based Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12030)
*Andrea Brunello,Cristian Curaba,Luca Geatti,Michele Mignani,Angelo Montanari,Nicola Saccomanno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对以一阶逻辑（FOL）为目标形式化的自动形式化研究，提出了统一的任务定义，并从本体抽取与逻辑翻译两个子任务视角对数据集、评测指标及基于大模型的方法进行了系统综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型重新激发了对自动形式化的兴趣，但当目标形式化为FOL时，该领域仍缺乏统一的任务表述与系统性综述；既有研究将本体抽取与逻辑翻译混为一谈，导致跨研究评测难以比较。

Method: 提出FOL自动形式化任务的原则性定义，明确区分本体抽取（Ontology Extraction）与逻辑翻译（Logical Translation）；在此基础上梳理现有数据集、评测指标与基于LLM的方法，涵盖微调、提示工程与基于验证的迭代精化。

Result: 系统归纳了现有资源与方法谱系，指出任务混同会掩盖跨研究评测的可比性问题，并明确了基准构建、语义评测、本体感知方法与端到端应用等方面的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该综述为FOL自动形式化提供了统一的任务框架与分类视角，有助于厘清评测口径并指引未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have renewed interest in autoformalization. Yet, when First-Order Logic (FOL) is considered as the target formalism, the field still lacks a unified task formulation and a systematic survey. This paper addresses this gap: we first provide a principled definition for the FOL-autoformalization task by distinguishing Ontology Extraction from Logical Translation, showing how their conflation obscures (cross-study) evaluation; we review existing datasets, evaluation metrics, and LLM-based methods, including fine-tuning, prompting, and verification-based refinement; we identify open challenges in benchmarking, semantic evaluation, ontology-aware methods, and end-to-end applications.

</details>


### [75] [ILM: An AI-Powered Storytelling Educational Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12064)
*Suhaila Mohammed,Abdelaziz Serour,Allison Lahnala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ILM 交互式教育平台，结合阿拉伯语 NLP、结构化知识表示与检索式问题生成，支持《先知故事》的叙事探索、理解与评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字平台虽提升了伊斯兰叙事的可及性，但对结构化学习与理解的支撑有限，尤其在阿拉伯语及多语言环境下。

Method: 由管理员审核的阿拉伯语叙事经知识图谱构建引擎抽取实体与叙事关系并结构化存储，支持可视化故事地图及基于实体/关系的提问；另设多语言检索管线，从原始叙事中检索相关段落以生成选择题与开放式理解题；开放题由 LLM-as-a-Judge 依据检索段落与参考答案判定正误；并以古兰经内容作为独立补充层。

Result: 系统实现并验证了结构化知识表示与检索式生成的结合，可跨阿拉伯语与多语言内容完成叙事探索、理解与评测；提供在线演示。

Conclusion: 结合结构化知识表示与检索式生成来支撑伊斯兰叙事的交互式学习具有可行性。

Abstract: Digital technologies have made Islamic narratives more accessible, but existing platforms provide limited support for structured learning and comprehension of these stories, particularly in Arabic and multilingual settings. We present ILM, an interactive educational platform for Stories of the Prophets that combines Arabic natural language processing, structured knowledge representation, and retrieval-based question generation. Admin-approved Arabic narratives are processed by a Knowledge Graph (KG) Constructor Engine that identifies entities and narrative relationships and stores them as structured knowledge, enabling learners to explore stories through a visual story map and answer entity- and relation-based questions generated from the KG. Separately, a multilingual retrieval pipeline retrieves relevant passages from the original narratives to generate multiple-choice and open-ended comprehension questions. For open-ended questions, an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluates learners' answers against the retrieved passages and reference answers to determine correctness. The platform also incorporates Quranic content as a separate enrichment layer, allowing selected narratives to be supplemented with source-supported information. By combining structured knowledge with passage-based retrieval, ILM supports narrative exploration, comprehension, and assessment across Arabic and multilingual content. The system demonstrates the feasibility of combining structured knowledge representation and retrieval-based generation to support interactive learning of Islamic narratives. A demo is available at anonymous.4open.science/r/mml-5FCF.

</details>


### [76] [All Verdicts are Not Equal: Rethinking LLM Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12083)
*Vineet Kumar,Darshita Rathore,Anindya Moitra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对LLM-as-a-Judge范式进行系统性可靠性审计，发现裁决在零温度下仍不可复现、受位置顺序影响，并存在"一致性高但错误"的假可靠现象；提出可信裁决率T统一指标，并验证整体性评分优于成对胜负率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为NLP评测的标准范式，但业界普遍将其视为确定性的"真值"，其系统性可靠性缺乏深入理解。作者希望揭示该范式在复现性、顺序不变性与准确性上的多重失效模式，并为其提供形式化度量与改进方向。

Method: 对六个前沿模型、四个基准、五种提示格式、两种呈现顺序、三种采样温度及每条件十次重复开展压力测试审计；提出统一指标"可信裁决率T"，其为评测可复现、顺序不变且准确的联合概率；基于T推导位置偏差导致的准确率理论上界，并比较成对胜负率与整体性评分两种评测方式。

Result: 在温度为零的相同重复下裁决仍会变化；位置顺序交换会翻转挑战性任务上的多数裁决；最具确定性的评判模型通过机械重复错误裁决获得完美一致性，但与真值一致率仅51%；可靠性呈条目特异性而非模型级别。

Conclusion: LLM-as-a-Judge存在严重且系统性的可靠性缺陷，单一模型层面的确定性并不等于可信。从成对胜负率转向整体性评分框架比任何单一提示格式干预都更能提升可信度，为稳健NLP评测提供可操作框架。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is the standard paradigm for NLP evaluation, yet its systemic reliability remains poorly understood despite being widely treated as a deterministic ground truth. We present a comprehensive reliability audit, stresstesting six frontier models across four benchmarks, five prompt formats, two presentation orders, three sampling temperatures, and ten repetitions per condition. Our empirical analysis reveals severe vulnerabilities: verdicts change across identical replications at temperature zero, position-order swaps flip the majority of verdicts on challenging tasks, and the most deterministic judge achieves perfect consistency by trivially repeating incorrect verdicts, agreeing with ground truth only 51% of the time. To formalize these multi-faceted failure modes, we introduce the trustworthy verdict rate (T ), a unified metric capturing the joint probability that an evaluation is reproducible, order-invariant, and accurate. UsingT , we derive a theoretical upper bound on accuracy imposed by position bias and show that reliability is item-specific rather than modellevel. Finally, we demonstrate that shifting from pairwise win-rate to holistic rubric scoring improves trustworthiness more than any single-format prompting intervention, offering an actionable framework for robust NLP evaluation.

</details>


### [77] [A persistent accuracy ceiling in automated verbal deception detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12118)
*Riccardo Loconte,Jonas Festor,Zane Fatjanova,Mariam Bolkvadze,Bennett Kleinberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统综述25年自动说谎检测文献（289篇报告、6,136个分类模型），并对97个数据集中的3,653个模型进行元分析。合并准确率74.4%（95% CI: 71.2%–77.4%），异质性显著。准确率主要由方法学质量驱动而非模型复杂度，嵌入与LLM的采用并未带来性能提升；仅12.46%报告使用可验证真值，仅23.96%模型在独立数据上评估。结果与人工方法元分析一致，提示70%–75%的准确率天花板难以被当前研究范式突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动说谎检测被视为克服人工言语欺骗检测局限的途径，但跨学科证据零散、结论不一，缺乏对25年研究的系统性整合与效应量估计，也未厘清性能提升究竟源于模型复杂度还是方法学质量。

Method: 系统性综述25年研究（289份报告、6,136个分类模型），并对嵌套于97个数据集中的3,653个模型进行元分析；以合并准确率为主要效应量，考察异质性，并分析方法学质量因素（真值可验证性、数据来源、类别平衡、评估程序）与模型复杂度（嵌入、大语言模型）对准确率的影响。

Result: 合并准确率为74.4%（95% CI: 71.2%–77.4%），异质性显著。准确率更多由方法学质量（真值、数据来源、类别平衡、评估程序）驱动，而非模型复杂度；采用嵌入与大语言模型并未带来预测性能提升。仅12.46%的报告使用了可验证真值的数据，仅23.96%的模型在独立数据上评估。

Conclusion: 自动说谎检测的合并准确率与人工方法的元分析结果相当，提示存在约70%–75%的性能天花板；该上限难以在现有研究惯例下被突破，未来需优先提升方法学质量（如可验证真值、独立评估），而非单纯追求模型复杂度。

Abstract: Automated methods have been proposed to overcome the limitations of human verbal deception detection, but evidence remains fragmented across disciplines. We systematically reviewed 25 years of research (289 reports, 6,136 classification models) and meta-analyzed 3,653 models nested within 97 datasets. Pooled accuracy was 74.4% (95% CI: 71.2%-77.4%) with substantial heterogeneity. Accuracy was driven by methodological quality (ground truth, data source, class balance, evaluation procedure) more than by model complexity: the adoption of embeddings and large language models has not translated into improved predictive performance. Only 12.46% of reports used data with verifiable ground-truth, and only 23.96% of models were evaluated on independent data. The pooled accuracy aligns with meta-analyses of manual approaches, suggesting a ceiling of 70-75%, unlikely to be lifted by current research conventions.

</details>


### [78] [Rehearse Everything, Remember Nothing: Attic-KV Rehearses What Will Be Read](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12133)
*Zhiyun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无训练KV缓存压缩方法Attic-KV，用自测式复习（引用上下文的问答对+自适应锚点）取代全上下文重读，在紧张预算下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流KV缓存压缩通过"重读上下文"并按注意力分数保留条目，假设复习越充分记忆越好。但低保留率下该假设失效：重读把预算摊薄到整个上下文，答案相关条目仅以略高于随机的概率被保留。

Method: 提出Attic-KV，一种无训练的自测式复习：让模型用引用上下文的问答对进行自我测验，并配合自适应数量的锚点token；遵循两点原则——复习将被读取的内容，复习量与内容量匹配。可插入基于梯度的KVgrad与已训练的RestoreKV+。

Result: 3%保留率下全上下文重读在RULER仅得31.5/96.5分，在LongBench自然文本任务上甚至低于不复习方法。Attic在8个设置中均为最佳无训练方法，插入KVgrad与RestoreKV+分别提升最多17.1与28.1分；3%保留率下领先全重读41.9分，且压缩速度快于全上下文重读。

Conclusion: KV缓存应"以测代读"：在紧预算下自测式复习优于全上下文重读，且在预算越紧时优势越大，兼具更快的压缩效率。

Abstract: Many key-value (KV) caches are compressed before anyone knows what will be asked of them: a document cached for retrieval, a prompt prefix shared across requests, the memory of a long conversation. The prevailing approach scores KV entries by rehearsal: the model rereads the context and keeps the entries it attends to, assuming that the more completely a cache rehearses its context, the better it remembers it. We show that under tight budgets this assumption backfires: rehearse everything, remember nothing. At a 3% keep ratio, rereading the whole context keeps 31.5 of 96.5 points on RULER, and on LongBench's natural-text tasks it falls below methods that rehearse nothing at all. The cause is that a cache keeps what it rehearses: rereading spreads the budget across the whole context, so the answer's own entries survive at little more than chance. Like a student before an exam, a cache remembers more by testing itself than by rereading. Two principles follow: rehearse what will be read, and rehearse as much as there is. We instantiate them as Attic-KV (Attic for short), a training-free rehearsal in which the model quizzes itself with question-answer pairs that quote the context, alongside anchor tokens in a content-adaptive amount. Changing only the rehearsal lifts three hosts that score it in three different ways: Attic alone is the best training-free method in all eight settings we test on RULER and LongBench's natural-text tasks, and plugged into the gradient-based KVgrad and the trained RestoreKV+, it raises them by up to 17.1 and 28.1 points. Its advantage grows as the budget shrinks, reaching 41.9 points over full rereading at a 3% keep ratio, and it compresses faster than rereading the whole context.

</details>


### [79] [Language-Specific Effects of Tokenizer Choice in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12144)
*Clara Meister,Gül Sena Altıntaş,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过训练123个语言模型、覆盖54种分词器，在架构、语料、训练token预算与优化均固定的条件下，系统研究分词器选择对不同语言影响的差异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词器选择会影响多语言语言建模，但词表容量有限，提升某些语言的表示往往以牺牲其他语言为代价。现有文献尚未回答"分词器选择是否在所有语言上同等重要"这一问题。

Method: 训练123个语言模型，覆盖54种分词器；在主要对比中固定模型架构、训练语料、训练token预算与优化设置，使模型仅分词器不同。分析各语言BPB标准差与其模型训练数据占比的相关性，并考察分词器训练数据中某语言被剔除、低资源语言获得更大分词器训练数据份额等条件下的影响，以及内在分词器属性与BPB的关联。

Result: 分词器选择对语言模型训练数据较少的语言影响更大：54种分词器间，某语言的BPB标准差随其模型训练数据份额下降而增大（31种训练语言Spearman rho=-0.52，28种有词边界的语言rho=-0.69）。将某语言排除在分词器训练之外会在所有研究语言中提高其BPB，且惩罚在模型训练数据较少的语言上往往更大。但让低资源语言在分词器训练数据中占更大份额并不无条件有利：等权分配以及与语言模型训练数据份额相反的反转分配都会提高其BPB，尤其在语言模型重复训练数据时。此外，与更好BPB相关的内在分词器属性因语言而异。

Conclusion: 使分词器"好"的因素取决于具体语言。量化这些属性的指标可用于预测下游模型间的BPB成对排名，为在训练语言模型之前筛选候选分词器提供了实用策略。

Abstract: Tokenizer choice affects multilingual language modeling, but vocabulary capacity is finite and vocabulary size is often constrained: improving representation for some languages often comes at the expense of others. We therefore ask whether tokenizer choice matters equally across languages, a question that the current literature leave unanswered. To this end, we train 123 language models spanning 54 tokenizers. In the main comparison, architecture, training corpus, training-token budget, and optimization are held fixed, so the models differ only in their tokenizer. We find that tokenizer choice matters more for languages with less language-model training data: across the 54 tokenizers, the standard deviation of a language's bits-per-byte (BPB) increases as its model training-data share decreases (Spearman rho = -0.52 over the 31 trained languages and -0.69 over the 28 written with word boundaries). Leaving a language out of tokenizer training raises its BPB in every language we study, and the penalty tends to be larger for languages with less language-model training data. Giving lower-resource languages a larger share of tokenizer-training data, however, does not unconditionally help those languages: both equal weighting and an allocation inverting the shares with respect to the language model training data increase their BPB, particularly when language-model training repeats data. Finally, which intrinsic tokenizer properties are associated with better BPB differs across languages, providing further evidence that what makes a good tokenizer depends on the language. We find that the metrics quantifying these properties can be successfully used to predict downstream models' pairwise BPB rankings, suggesting a practical strategy for screening tokenizer candidates before training language models.

</details>


### [80] [SciTBERT: A family of chronologically consistent language models for scientific and technological language processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12207)
*Thomas Gebhart,Russell J. Funk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciTBERT系列模型（含SciTBERT-CI），通过按年份截断（2013–2025）的训练语料构建时间一致性的领域编码器，并发布PatRepEval基准，验证时间约束下的编码器在科学-技术界面的分类、回归与检索任务中可匹配或超越无时间约束的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer已广泛用于科技进展研究，针对论文/专利微调的编码器在下游任务上优于通用模型。但这类模型存在"前视偏差"（lookahead bias）与领域偏差：训练语料的时间与文本来源分布不受约束，导致其在研究科学、技术及其交界处的时间依赖性或档案性属性时适用性受限。

Method: 1）构建SciTBERT：一系列时间一致的BERT衍生语言模型，训练语料涵盖科学论文、专利及高质量教育类网页文本，训练数据截断日期覆盖2013至2025年间的每一年；2）以时间一致方式，利用论文与专利引用进行后训练，得到SciTBERT-CI系列；3）提出PatRepEval基准，包含面向科学-技术界面的专利相关文本嵌入任务套件。

Result: 即使受早期年份语料限制导致训练数据有限，SciTBERT模型总体仍优于此前的领域专用编码器；在PatRepEval覆盖论文与专利的分类、回归与检索任务中，表现表明编码器表示与下游任务领域分布对齐的重要性，且时间一致编码器可匹配甚至超过无时间约束训练的模型。

Conclusion: 时间一致的预训练与后训练范式可有效缓解前视与领域偏差，为科技进展的时间依赖性与档案性研究提供更可靠的编码器，并强调了表示-任务领域分布对齐的关键作用。

Abstract: Pre-trained transformer models are increasingly being used to study scientific and technological progress. Encoders tuned to paper or patent text outperform general-purpose models on downstream classification, regression, and proximity tasks within science and technology. However, the applicability of these models for studying time-dependent or archival properties of science, technology, and their interface is limited due to lookahead and domain biases inherent to these pre-trained models. These limitations arise from training on corpora with unconstrained chronological and text source distributions. We introduce SciTBERT: a family of chronologically consistent BERT-derived language models trained on text from scientific papers, patents, and high-quality educational web text with training data cutoff dates spanning each year between 2013 and 2025. We also post-train these models in a chronologically-consistent manner using paper and patent citations, creating SciTBERT-CI model family. We find that these models generally outperform predecessor domain-specific encoder models even when training data is limited by early year restrictions in the corpus. To further investigate the extent to which this class of models can learn representations that bridge science and technology, we introduce the PatRepEval benchmark, a suite of patent-related text embedding tasks at the science-technology interface. Performance in a variety of classification, regression, and retrieval tasks spanning papers and patents highlights the importance of aligning encoder model representations with the domain distributions of their downstream tasks, and chronologically consistent encoders can match or exceed models trained without temporal constraints.

</details>


### [81] [Language Models as AI Research World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12235)
*Zijun Wang,Zewen Liu,Minhua Lin,Zhaotian Weng,Zhan Shi,Bing He,Yisi Sang,Dakuo Wang,Benoit Dumoulin,Wei Jin,Yuyin Zhou,Cihang Xie,Hanqing Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型作为"研究世界模型"(RWM)，用于预测科研实验结果，并证明真实实验经验能显著提升其对未见干预的预测与筛选能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科研智能体提出实验的能力已超过其在真实环境中执行实验的能力，在实验预算有限时，结果预测成为持续自我改进的关键能力。

Method: 基于9个研究环境（涵盖预训练、后训练与推理）、2600余条实验记录（超171,000 H100 GPU小时）评估语言模型作为RWM；比较零经验、同环境经验与跨环境经验三种设定，并在固定选择预算下进行多轮Autoresearch实验，同时对13个语言模型作消融。

Result: 真实实验经验可提升同环境未见干预的预测（Spearman +0.27）并可跨环境迁移：仅用OLMo3、Marin、Nanochat的预训练经验，即可使Qwen3环境的选择遗憾降低78%；多轮Autoresearch中，同环境与跨环境知识分别使最优增益提升15.8%与11.6%；消融显示加入研究知识对干预排序的提升超过更换模型或单纯增加推理努力。

Conclusion: 语言模型可作为研究世界模型，积累了实验数据的RWM能更有效预测与筛选实验，因此值得持续积累实验数据以支持未来RWM训练。

Abstract: AI research agents automate the cycle of proposing, implementing, and evaluating experiments, opening a path toward recursive self-improvement. Yet their ability to propose experiments outpaces their capacity to execute them in real environments, making outcome prediction a key capability for sustained self-improvement under limited experimental budgets. We investigate language models as Research World Models (RWMs), which predict the outcomes of candidate interventions across research environments. Our evaluation draws on over 2,600 experimental records from nine research environments spanning pretraining, post-training, and inference, representing more than 171,000 H100 GPU-hours of experimentation. Research knowledge acquired from real experimental experience improves RWM predictions of unseen interventions within the same environment (Spearman +0.27), and can be reused across environments. For example, using only pretraining experience from OLMo3, Marin, and Nanochat, an RWM reduces selection regret in the Qwen3 environment by 78% compared with zero-experience setting. These benefits extend to multi-round Autoresearch under a fixed selection budget: RWMs with in-env and cross-env research knowledge increase the best gain achieved by 15.8% and 11.6%, respectively. Ablations across 13 language models used as RWMs show that adding research knowledge can improve intervention ranking more than changing models or increasing reasoning effort alone. These findings support language models as RWMs and motivate accumulating experimental data for future RWM training.

</details>


### [82] [TokenRouter: Efficient Serving System for Token-Level LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12242)
*Tianyu Fu,Tengxuan Liu,Ruoxi Wang,Yixin Dong,Yi Ge,Yichen You,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokenRouter 是一个面向 token 级路由 LLM 推理的高效、易用的服务系统，通过请求中心编程与模型中心执行分离、子服务器异步调度及基于吞吐模型的延迟批处理，实现了解码吞吐 2.01-64.15 倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 路由可跨模型分配推理负载，推进成本-质量帕累托前沿。会话或查询级粗粒度路由已在生产系统广泛采用，而近期算法研究表明 token 级细粒度路由能带来显著的效率与质量收益。但现有系统基于单模型假设，在 token 级路由下会出现严重的步进失同步与频繁的批准入延迟，且对开发者实现复杂度高。

Method: 提出 TokenRouter 服务系统，遵循"请求中心编程、模型中心执行"原则：开发者从单一请求视角描述路由逻辑，运行时为每个 LLM 启动一个子服务器并异步派发请求；每个子服务器采用延迟批处理调度器，其最优超参数由系统吞吐数学模型推导得出。

Result: 在多种路由算法、工作负载与模型组合下，TokenRouter 的解码吞吐比现有系统高 2.01-64.15 倍，显著提升了 token 级 LLM 路由的服务效率。代码已开源。

Conclusion: TokenRouter 有效解决了 token 级路由推理中的步进失同步与批准入延迟问题，兼顾效率与开发者友好性，大幅推进了 token 级 LLM 路由的服务效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing distributes inference work across different models, advancing the cost-quality Pareto frontier of LLM serving. While coarse-grained routing at the session or query level has been widely adopted in production systems, recent algorithmic work shows that fine-grained token-level routing can yield substantial efficiency and quality gains. However, efficiently serving token-level routed inference poses significant challenges to existing systems. Built on single-LLM assumptions, current systems suffer from severe step desynchronization and frequent batch admission delays under token-level routing, and they also impose high implementation complexity on developers. To address these challenges, we design TokenRouter, an efficient and developer-friendly serving system for token-level routed LLM inference. TokenRouter follows the principle of request-centric programming, model-centric execution: developers describe routing logic from the perspective of a single request, while the runtime launches a subserver for each LLM and dispatches requests asynchronously. Each subserver employs a delayed-batching scheduler, whose optimal hyperparameters are derived from a mathematical throughput model of the system. Across diverse routing algorithms, workloads, and model pairs, TokenRouter achieves 2.01-64.15x higher decoding throughput than existing systems, substantially advancing the serving efficiency of token-level LLM routing. Our code is available at https://github.com/thu-nics/TokenRouter.

</details>


### [83] [EgoVoice: Proactive Spoken Assistance from Egocentric Multimodal Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12248)
*Heeseung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 EgoVoice 框架，用于训练与评估主动式第一人称语音助手，通过 HoloAssist 视频构建数据并微调全模态 LLM，结合 DPO 改进主动干预的时机与内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴 AR 助手需在连续第一人称视音频流中被动感知用户活动并主动提供语音指导，但现有主动视频助手、口语对话系统与第一人称任务理解研究相互独立，尚未解决"何时说话、说什么"的联合决策问题。

Method: 基于 HoloAssist 中真实人类指导者的录像，通过音源分离与语音重合成构建干净音频流，将每段视频会话转换为"逐时刻决定保持沉默或给出语音指导"的格式；用该数据微调全模态 LLM，并用直接偏好优化（DPO）进一步改善其主动干预行为。

Result: 在闭源与开源模型上的实验表明，现有系统很少能产生时机恰当、内容有意义的主动干预；EgoVoice 相较零样本骨干模型在干预时机、内容相关性和人类偏好上均有明显提升。

Conclusion: EgoVoice 为主动式第一人称语音助手提供了可训练、可评估的统一框架，证明通过数据构建、微调与 DPO 能有效提升连续流场景下的主动干预质量。

Abstract: Wearable augmented reality (AR) assistants are moving toward continuous real-world interaction, where they perceive the user's activity through first-person video and audio and provide timely spoken guidance without being explicitly asked. While proactive video assistants, spoken dialog systems, and egocentric task understanding have each advanced rapidly, existing systems do not address the joint problem of deciding when to speak and what to say from continuous first-person streams. We introduce EgoVoice, a framework for training and evaluating proactive egocentric spoken assistants. From HoloAssist video recordings of real human instructors, we construct clean audio streams through source separation and speech resynthesis, and convert each video session into a format where the model must decide at each moment whether to remain silent or provide spoken guidance. We fine-tune an omni-modal LLM with our data, and further improve its proactive intervention behavior with direct preference optimization. Experiments across closed and open-source models show that existing systems rarely produce well-timed, meaningful proactive interventions, while EgoVoice yields clear improvements in intervention timing, content relevance, and human preference over the zero-shot backbone.

</details>


### [84] [HarnessSQL: Harness-Native Training for SQL Agents in Realistic Database Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12274)
*Haolin Yang,Jipeng Zhang,Jian Xie,Shuaishuai Gong,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HarnessSQL，一种在交互式SQL执行环境中进行后训练（SFT+RL）的框架，显著提升小模型在多轮数据库任务中的执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL模型通常直接学习问题到静态查询的映射，而真实的数据库智能体需要在有状态、多轮交互中操作数据库（检查schema、执行探测查询、诊断错误、修正假设）。执行harness仅在推理时引入，导致训练与部署之间存在严重不匹配。

Method: HarnessSQL在监督微调和强化学习全过程中保留完整交互结构：构建隔离的可执行数据库环境并配备隐藏的执行oracle；让teacher模型直接在目标SQL harness中rollout；仅保留经过验证的轨迹用于全序列SFT，随后进行执行奖励RL。

Result: 在Spider 2.0-SQLite上大幅提升小模型执行准确率：Qwen3-8B从15.5%升至45.2%，Qwen3-14B从22.2%升至54.8%；并能有效迁移到分布外交互式基准，如BIRD-Interact和LiveSQLBench。

Conclusion: 直接在执行harness中训练数据库智能体，对于掌握复杂、长程的数据库工作流至关重要。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL models are commonly trained to map questions directly to static queries, whereas real-world database agents operate through stateful, multi-turn interaction with live databases -- inspecting schemas, executing probe queries, diagnosing errors, and revising hypotheses. This creates a critical train-deploy mismatch, as the execution harness that mediates this interaction is introduced only at inference time. To bridge this gap, we propose HarnessSQL, a harness-native post-training framework that preserves the full interaction structure throughout both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. HarnessSQL builds isolated, executable database environments paired with hidden execution oracles, rolls out teachers directly inside the target SQL harness, and retains only verified trajectories for full-sequence SFT, followed by execution-reward RL. Across Spider 2.0-SQLite, HarnessSQL dramatically boosts the execution accuracy of compact models, raising Qwen3-8B from 15.5% to 45.2% and Qwen3-14B from 22.2% to 54.8%, while transferring effectively to out-of-distribution interactive benchmarks such as BIRD-Interact and LiveSQLBench. Our findings demonstrate that training database agents directly within their execution harness is essential for mastering complex, long-horizon database workflows.

</details>


### [85] [VFold: Symmetry-Aware Cross-Layer Value Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12338)
*Neha Verma,Sungwon Kim,Kenton Murray,Kevin Duh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出对称感知的value cache合并策略，降低KV cache显存占用，避免性能退化与架构改动，可与量化、key剪枝叠加达到更高压缩比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM解码时长上下文下KV cache占用显存过大，现有层间相似性压缩方法多需改动模型架构并带来显著开销，亟需一种无需架构改动、无损性能的压缩方案。

Method: 利用层间缓存相似性，设计对称感知（symmetry-aware）的value cache合并策略，在解码过程中合并value状态以降低缓存内存，且不引入架构改动。

Result: 在避免性能明显退化和解码期架构开销的同时降低缓存内存；可与高压缩比量化或key cache剪枝组合，达到单一方法无法企及的压缩率，且额外成本极小。

Conclusion: 揭示了value cache中被长期低估的容量冗余，为显存受限下扩展上下文窗口提供了简单而高效的方向。

Abstract: While caching key-value (KV) states accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) decoding, this cache can dominate memory usage at long context lengths. One solution is to compress this memory by exploiting inter-layer cache similarities. However, most existing techniques necessitate architectural changes to LLMs and incur substantial overhead. In this work, we propose a symmetry-aware value cache merging strategy that reduces cache memory while avoiding both harmful performance degradation and architectural overhead during decoding. Furthermore, we show that this approach can be exploited alongside existing cache compression techniques, composing with high-ratio quantization or key cache pruning to reach compression ratios that neither method reaches alone, with minimal additional cost. Ultimately, our findings reveal a major source of underutilized capacity in the value cache, offering a simple yet highly effective direction for scaling context windows under memory constraints.

</details>


### [86] [Which Skill to Distill? SGUID: Selecting a Compact Skill Bank for Model-Skill Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12367)
*Yuhan Liu,Xiyao Ma,Zhongkai Sun,Xu Han,Chengyuan Ma,Benjamin Z. Yao,Chenlei Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出按语义相关性检索的技能大部分在蒸馏中无益（少于25%提供有效信号），提出SGUID方法，依据训练中是否持续产生有效学习信号来筛选少量技能用于蒸馏，从而实现稳定的模型-技能协同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过语义相关性从技能库中检索技能，并将其作为推理期补丁或用于模型蒸馏，但忽略了每个技能各自的效用。作者发现，在on-policy蒸馏中，将技能条件策略作为教师时，少于25%的检索技能能提供有用的蒸馏信号，说明技能筛选是被忽视的关键环节。

Method: 提出SGUID方法：仅保留在训练中持续产生有效学习信号的技能，从而选出一个紧凑的技能子集用于蒸馏。该方法支持模型-技能协同演化循环：先蒸馏选定技能得到更新模型，再从更新模型的rollout中构建新的候选技能库，并由SGUID选择下一轮要内化的技能。

Result: 在Olmo和Qwen家族共四个模型上，蒸馏6个精选技能即可在四个模型中的三个上匹配或超过全库蒸馏的平均avg@12；经过第二轮蒸馏3个新选技能的循环后，四个模型全部达到或超过全库蒸馏，而全库规模最多是其11倍。第二轮循环将Qwen3-8B从64.3%提升至66.3%。在Qwen3-4B上，不加筛选地更新模型会导致性能下降（HMMT25下降0.3个百分点），而SGUID在第一轮将HMMT25提升0.5个百分点、第二轮提升1.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 并非所有技能都值得蒸馏；技能选择是实现稳定模型-技能协同演化的关键机制。SGUID通过筛选持续有效的技能，以远小于全库的规模获得相当或更优的性能，并保证多轮演化的稳定性。

Abstract: Skills, reusable procedural guidance added at inference, can substantially improve LLM downstream performance (Li et al., 2026). Prior work retrieves skills from a bank by semantic relevance, then uses them as inference-time patches or for model distillation. The individual utility of each skill, however, is largely neglected. We first show that, in on-policy distillation where skill-conditioned policies serve as teachers, fewer than 25% of retrieved skills provide useful distillation signals. We then propose SGUID, a method for selecting a compact subset of skills for distillation. SGUID retains a skill only if it consistently yields effective learning signals during training. The selected skills are then distilled to produce a better model. Our results show that not all skills are worth distilling. Across four models from the Olmo and Qwen families, distilling 6 selected skills matches or exceeds full-bank distillation in mean avg@12 on three of the four models, and on all four after a second round that distills 3 newly selected skills, while the full banks are up to 11x larger. Importantly, SGUID supports stable model-skill co-evolution: after a distillation round, a new candidate bank is curated from the updated model's rollouts, and SGUID selects which skills to internalize next. In the second round, this loop selects 3 new skills and improves Qwen3-8B from 64.3% to 66.3%. The selection step is essential for stability: on Qwen3-4B, naively updating the model with unfiltered skills degrades performance, including a 0.3 percentage point drop on HMMT25, whereas SGUID improves HMMT25 by 0.5 points after the first round and 1.1 points after the second. These results identify skill selection as the key mechanism for stable model-skill co-evolution.

</details>


### [87] [Latent Core Tokenizer: Compress, but Meaningfully](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12376)
*Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Millicent Ochieng,Ogbemi Ekwejunor-Etchie,Ade Famoti,Jacki O'Neill,Debjit Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Latent Core Tokenizer (LCT)，将结构发现与词表构建解耦，在多语言场景下优于BPE、Unigram及parity-aware BPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分词器多以压缩率为优化目标，但紧凑词表未必能在不同语言间均匀分配容量，压缩率无法预测表征质量。

Method: LCT为语言无关方法，先利用最小描述长度（MDL）、基于熵的边界信号与形态句法约束识别可复用语言单元，再构建共享词表。

Result: 在104种语言、200K词表下，LCT的fertility更低、MorphScore更高，跨语言分词成本差异与基线相当；四个多语言下游基准上总分较BPE、Unigram、parity-aware BPE分别提升1.48、1.83、2.00分。

Conclusion: 压缩率本身不能预测表征质量，形态驱动的结构发现以及频率在跨语言词表分配中的作用至关重要。

Abstract: Tokenizers are commonly optimized for compression, but a compact vocabulary does not necessarily distribute its capacity evenly across languages. We introduce the Latent Core Tokenizer (LCT), a language-agnostic approach that separates structural discovery from vocabulary construction. LCT uses Minimum Description Length, entropy-based boundary signals, and morphotactic constraints to identify reusable linguistic units before constructing a shared vocabulary. Across 104 languages with a 200K-token vocabulary, LCT achieves lower fertility and higher MorphScore than BPE, Unigram, and parity-aware BPE, while maintaining comparable cross-lingual disparity in tokenization cost. Across four multilingual downstream benchmarks, LCT improves aggregate score by 1.48, 1.83, and 2.00 points over BPE, Unigram, and parity-aware BPE, respectively. Our findings show that compression alone does not predict representation quality and highlight the importance of morphology-driven structural discovery and how frequency is used to allocate the final vocabulary across languages.

</details>


### [88] [Predicting Alignment Generalization with Value Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12410)
*Andy Liu,Mehar Bhatia,Karolina Stanczak,Mona Diab,Vered Shwartz,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“对齐泛化预测”任务，即预测将模型微调以遵循某一价值后，其在大量未见过价值上的行为变化，并通过66种价值的大规模分析验证基于激活的表征显著优于基于文本描述的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管当前后训练模型在对齐评估中得分很高，但在狭窄行为集合上训练仍会以意外方式影响模型在未见情境中的行为，因此需要理解和预测对齐目标的泛化效应。

Method: 对现代对齐目标中的66种价值进行大规模对齐泛化效应分析，构建泛化矩阵，并基准测试表征技术；比较基于模型激活的表征与基于价值文本描述的方法，并将预测泛化的表征用于衡量多价值对齐目标中价值相似性及构建价值分类体系。

Result: 基于激活的最佳方法与泛化矩阵的相关性达到0.45，而基于描述的基线仅为0.05；预测对齐泛化的表征可用于衡量多价值对齐目标中价值的相似性，且与模型鲁棒性显著相关；还发现存在共享的、与模型无关的价值空间的初步证据。

Conclusion: 研究强调了对LLM价值泛化进行建模的重要性，并表明该工作可用于更经验化地设计和训练模型行为，同时提出了首个基于经验泛化动态的LLM价值分类体系。

Abstract: LLM developers post-train their models to exhibit prosocial values and behavioral traits, which are enumerated in an alignment target. However, while recent post-training developments have yielded models that score highly on alignment evaluations, training models on sets of narrow behaviors still influences their behavior across unseen contexts and environments in unexpected ways. In this paper, we establish the task of alignment generalization prediction, i.e., predicting how fine-tuning a model to follow a given value changes its behavior across a wide range of held-out values. We conduct a large-scale analysis of alignment generalization effects across 66 values found in modern alignment targets, and benchmark representational techniques on the alignment generalization prediction task. We find that representations based on model activations when applying values in context significantly outperform methods based on textual descriptions of the values. Specifically, the best activations-based methods achieve correlations of 0.45 with our generalization matrix, compared with 0.05 from description-based baselines. We then show the applicability of representations that predict alignment generalization toward downstream tasks by using them to measure how similar the values in a multi-value alignment target are, which we find is significantly correlated with model robustness. Finally, we show initial evidence towards a shared, model-independent value space, which we use to develop the first taxonomy of LLM values grounded in empirical generalization dynamics. Our work demonstrates the importance of studying value generalization in LLMs and its application toward the more empirical design and training of model behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [An Explainable Header-Centric Framework for Large-Scale Semantic Table Interpretation and Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10541)
*Marcelo Valentim Silva,Hannes Herrmann,Valerie Maxville*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以表头为中心的可解释框架，在"仅元数据"条件下完成列类型标注（CTA）与数据质量评估（DQA），覆盖约12万表头列，并给出质量指标HeadersIQ与KG映射路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱质量不仅取决于下游图验证，也取决于集成前表元数据的质量；在仅元数据、单元格值缺失或不可用的语义表格解释场景中，表头成为关键语义证据，但缺乏可追溯、可解释的处理流程。

Method: 将表头映射到39个可解释的FinalFormat类型（基于人工整理词汇资源），并通过SourceKeywords保留token级溯源；每个类型激活基于数据质量问题（DQI）分类法的验证规则，检测缺值、重复、域违规、类型错误、时序不匹配等；检测结果聚合为轻量无权重数据源级质量指标HeadersIQ；另设并行的KG映射路径对齐DBpedia与Schema.org。

Result: 在UCI、Prague、Kaggle、VizNet/Sato、SOTAB、T2Dv2及SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG等异构基准（约120,000表头列）上评估，对噪声真实元数据具有较广的实用覆盖；SemTab 2024官方GT-strict评测结果一般，但盲审诊断审计显示多数不匹配源于基准粒度、别名与本体选择效应，而非表头预测完全不合理。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可复用的流程，用于元数据驱动的语义标注、数据源级质量监测与面向知识图谱的基准诊断；诊断审计仅作为分歧模式的证据，不作为修正后的基准性能。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph (KG) quality depends not only on downstream graph validation, but also on the quality of tabular metadata used before integration. In metadata-only Semantic Table Interpretation (STI), where cell values are unavailable, noisy, or unsuitable, column headers become a critical source of semantic evidence for traceable KG preparation.
  We present an explainable, header-centric framework for metadata-only Column Type Annotation (CTA) and Data Quality Assessment (DQA). The framework maps headers to 39 interpretable FinalFormat types using curated lexical resources and preserves token-level traceability through SourceKeywords. Each assigned type activates validation rules based on a taxonomy of Data Quality Issues (DQIs), producing detections such as missing data, duplicates, domain violations, wrong data type, and temporal mismatch. These detections are aggregated into HeadersIQ, a lightweight, unweighted data source-level quality metric.
  The framework was evaluated across heterogeneous benchmarks, including UCI, Prague, Kaggle, VizNet/Sato, SOTAB, T2Dv2, and the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, comprising around 120,000 header columns. The results show broad practical coverage across noisy real-world metadata, while a parallel KG-mapping pathway supports alignment to DBpedia and Schema.org. On the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, the official GT-strict evaluation was modest. However, a blinded diagnostic audit indicates that many mismatches reflect benchmark granularity, aliasing, and ontology-selection effects rather than wholly implausible header-centric predictions. We report this audit as diagnostic evidence on disagreement patterns, not as revised benchmark performance. Overall, the paper presents a reusable workflow for metadata-driven semantic annotation, data source-level quality monitoring, and KG-oriented benchmark diagnosis.

</details>


### [90] [Synthesis Through Simulation: Generating Coherent Enterprise Data via Scalable Agent-System Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10549)
*Yipeng Li,Ashutosh Hathidara,Jane Lo,Harshavardhan Abichandani,Gunraj Singh,Atin Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Synthesis Through Simulation (STS)，一种免 schema 的数据合成范式：LLM 智能体在策略约束的模拟企业环境中调用 API 生成数据，并配以领域无关的 Generalist Populator (GP)。GP 在十个环境中实现 0.88 平均边际保真度与 100% 约束满足率且无需 DB schema。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级工具调用智能体的规模化训练与评估受商业和法律限制（企业系统、数据、数据库 schema 不可获取）严重制约。表格式数据合成受结构有效性与 schema 可得性限制，而基于流程的方法虽保证有效性却缺乏分布保真度且需逐域人工编写。作者希望同时解决结构有效性、分布保真度与合成可扩展性三者的矛盾。

Method: 提出 STS 范式：LLM 智能体在模拟企业环境中对 policy-enforcing API 执行操作来生成数据。由于数据由定义有效性的同一环境生成，结构有效性由构造保证；同时将有效性约束与分布建模解耦，使两者可独立处理。配套设计领域无关智能体 Generalist Populator (GP) 负责分布保真度与扩展性。

Result: GP 在全部十个环境上达到 0.88 平均边际保真度与 100% 约束满足率，且无 DB schema 访问权限；统计型合成器因必需种子数据需求在七个环境中不适用；具备 schema 权限的智能体在 airline 环境紧耦合工作流上因任务组合脆弱而失败率达 82% 轨迹。

Conclusion: STS 通过"由环境构造有效性"的思路，在免 schema 条件下兼顾结构有效性与分布保真度，优于统计型与 schema 特权型基线。作者开源完整框架、十个环境及生成数据集。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents have become central to enterprise AI, yet training and evaluating them at scale remains severely constrained due to business and legal restrictions on enterprise systems, data, and database schemas. Tabular data synthesis offers a natural alternative, but its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by structural validity and schema availability, while procedure-based approaches yield the opposite weakness, typically lacking distributional fidelity without per-domain authoring. We introduce **Synthesis Through Simulation** (STS), a **schema--free** data synthesis paradigm in which an LLM agent generates data by executing operations against policy-enforcing APIs within simulated enterprise environments. Because data is generated through the same environment that defines what is valid, STS guarantees structural validity by construction while decoupling validity enforcement from distribution modeling, allowing each to be addressed independently. The **Generalist Populator** (GP), STS's domain-agnostic agent, addresses the remaining challenges of distributional fidelity and synthesis scalability: GP achieves **0.88** average marginal fidelity and **100\% constraint satisfaction** across all ten environments *without access to DB schemas*, while statistical synthesizers are inapplicable to seven due to necessary seed data requirements, and schema-privileged agents fail 82\% of trajectories on airline environment's tightly coupled workflows due to brittle task composition. We open-source the full framework, all ten environments, and generated datasets at https://github.com/SAP/synthesis-through-simulation.

</details>


### [91] [Agent-Controlled Forgetting for Tool-Using Agents: Reversible Context Curation in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10590)
*Jan-Peter Franke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究“智能体可控遗忘”：模型选择既往工具结果，在原位替换为简短摘要并归档原文以便恢复，在噪声工具使用轨迹中显著节省上下文与成本，但效果依赖工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用智能体的历史观测中，有用内容往往远小于原始载荷，导致提示词上下文冗余膨胀，带来token与API成本浪费。作者希望在不丢失可恢复性的前提下让智能体主动管理自身上下文。

Method: 提出智能体可控遗忘机制：由行为模型选取此前观测到的工具结果，在每个原位置替换为一条短note，并将精确原文保存在可恢复归档中。用Python harness提供批量归档与显式恢复，无需任务特定模型训练，同时保护用户指令与助手消息不被操作。

Result: 在探索性OpenTelemetry调试案例加一个无关实现任务中，方法结束时provider-reported提示词token为231,951，而保留完整历史为912,492；累计输入token减少50%，估计API成本USD 1.28–1.44，对照约USD 4.38。两种设置均通过两案例主行为oracle，但都未完全满足后续评估。方法请求次数更多，耗时增加17%。另一应用开发任务对未产生上下文或成本节省，且更早的续写虽减少上下文但人工评估质量更低。

Conclusion: 在噪声工具使用轨迹中可实现可观的资源节省，证明可逆上下文管理的潜力；但工作负载依赖性是核心考量，需针对任务类型判断是否适用。

Abstract: Tool-using agents repeatedly carry observations whose useful content can be much smaller than their original payload. We study agent-controlled forgetting: the acting model selects previously observed tool results, replaces each with a short note at its original position, and retains the exact original in a recoverable archive. A Python harness exposes batch archival and explicit recovery without task-specific model training, while protecting user instructions and assistant messages from these operations. In an exploratory OpenTelemetry debugging case followed by an unrelated implementation task, the method ended with 231,951 provider-reported prompt tokens versus 912,492 under retained history, used 50% fewer cumulative input tokens, and had an estimated API cost of USD 1.28-1.44 versus approximately USD 4.38. Both arms passed the two-case primary behavioral oracle; neither fully satisfied the follow-up evaluation. The method made more requests and took 17% longer. A contrasting application-development pair produced no context or cost saving, and an earlier continuation exhibited lower manually assessed quality despite reduced context. These observations demonstrate substantial resource savings in noisy tool-use trajectories and identify workload dependence as a central consideration for reversible context management.

</details>


### [92] [Verification and Self-Improvement in Agentic AI: Foundations and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10611)
*Chien-Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"有界验证"框架，通过隐藏终端随机性，将智能体AI的性能提升机制（搜索更久、额外支持、修改提议/验证方式）区分开来；刻画了原生可达（native reach）与闭包前沿（closure frontier）两类语言，并证明其在多数放大、存在接受、精确验证等情形下的性质，分析自指改进与选择误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统性能分数无法区分提升性能的具体机制——是搜索更久、获得额外支持，还是改变了提议与验证输出的方式。作者希望建立一个形式化框架，把"自我改进"的声明与其在正确性、可采纳证据、验证资源和选择误差上的义务对应起来。

Method: 引入"阶段（stage）"概念：规定可采纳的转录、多项式界、交替式验证协议和终端检查器。区分原生可达（使用默认支持）与闭包前沿（允许接口已承认的所有支持）。在逐点概率间隙和任务相对可靠性假设下定义语言。比较独立多数放大、随机带上的存在接受、精确验证（零随机）等机制，并分析随机验证器类与多项式层级的关系、表示分析与递归自我改进。

Result: 证明独立多数放大保持两类语言，而随机带上的存在接受可能接受错误输出。精确验证为零随机特例，给出位置与完备性结果。随机验证器类满足 Σ_k^P ⊆ Σ_k^RV ⊆ Σ_{k+1}^P；严格扩大与深度分离需要显式复杂度假设，而 BPP=P 时精确伴随类具有相同前沿。表示分析区分了不变接受与在重构下会变化的核心/支持标签。在公共可靠解释器与固定验证协议下，一致有界的自我修改仍留在同一验证类内。另设条件误差预算控制对自适应候选的误选，配额强制的XOR合成族将搜索成功率的无界比率与被接受语言的变化分离开。

Conclusion: 该框架将AI系统的自我改进声明转化为对四类义务的约束：正确性、可采纳证据、验证资源与选择误差，从而可由性能分数之外的形式化审计检验改进机制。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems can improve by searching longer, receiving additional support, or modifying how they propose and verify outputs. A performance score does not distinguish these mechanisms. We compare these changes through bounded verification with hidden terminal randomness. A stage specifies admissible transcripts, polynomial bounds, an alternating verification protocol, and a terminal checker. Its native reach uses default support; its closure frontier permits all support already admitted by the interface. Under a uniform pointwise probability gap and task-relative soundness, these are well-defined languages. We prove that independent majority amplification preserves both languages, whereas existential acceptance over random tapes can admit incorrect outputs. Exact verification is the zero-randomness case, with placement and completeness results. The randomized-verifier classes satisfy $Σ_k^{\mathrm{P}}\subseteqΣ_k^{\mathrm{RV}}\subseteqΣ_{k+1}^{\mathrm{P}}$; strict enlargement and depth separation require explicit complexity assumptions, while $\mathrm{BPP}=\mathrm{P}$ yields exact companions with the same frontiers. Representation analysis separates invariant acceptance from core-versus-support labels that can change under refactoring. For recursive self-improvement, uniformly bounded self-modification under a common sound interpreter and fixed verification protocol remains within the same verification class. A separate conditional-error budget controls false selection across adaptively chosen candidates. A quota-enforced XOR-synthesis family separates unbounded ratios of search success from changes in the accepted languages; exact and probabilistic audits check the resulting evidence requirements. The framework ties self-improvement claims to obligations on correctness, admissible evidence, verification resources, and selection error.

</details>


### [93] [Reading the Room: Foundations, Design, and Challenges of Normative Competence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10906)
*Andrea Wynn,Harsh Satija,Seokhyun,Baek,Anqi Liu,Eric Nalisnick,Gillian K. Hadfield*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文首次提出并评估大语言模型的“规范能力”（normative competence），即仅通过交互推断社区所执行的规范的能力。作者构建了受合成规范约束的多智能体社区辩论环境，发现基线LLM智能体即使在学习规范能提升准确率时也学不会规范；规范模块的效果高度依赖规范风格与模型能力；面对附带的非规范性个体行为时，智能体表现出“无选择性归因失败”，不加区分地模仿噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社区由规范系统治理，AI对齐需要让系统遵循这些规范。但规范数量庞大、变化迅速且常具任意性（如着装、语言惯例），依赖静态预训练知识无法应对，因此对齐要求模型具备“规范能力”——仅从交互中辨别社区所执行的规范。现有工作尚未对LLM的规范能力进行操作化和评估。

Method: 提出一个受合成规范控制辩论准入的多智能体社区辩论设定，将规范能力与预训练暴露隔离开来，从而孤立地研究规范学习。在此基础上测试基线LLM智能体，并实验多种“规范模块”（用于规范推断的架构组件）；同时引入伴随真实规范的个体性、非规范行为，考察智能体的归因是否具有选择性，并检验在明确惩罚模仿多余行为时该模式是否持续。

Result: 基线LLM智能体无法学会规范，即使学习规范本可提升其准确率。规范遵循对规范风格和驱动规范模块的模型能力高度敏感，显示缺乏泛化性。当真实规范伴随个体性非规范行为时，LLM智能体出现无选择性归因失败：不加区分地连同被执行的规则一起复制个体性噪声；即使明确惩罚模仿不必要行为，该模式依然存在。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个对LLM规范能力进行操作化和评估的研究，表明当前AI系统擅长行为模仿，却缺乏辨别社会所执行秩序的能力，凸显了规范能力作为对齐核心挑战的地位。

Abstract: Human communities are governed by normative systems: shared standards that produce \textit{norms} dictating acceptable behavior, enforced through community sanctioning. Aligning increasingly autonomous AI systems with these norms is a central alignment challenge, complicated by the fact that norms are vast in number, change quickly, and are often arbitrary (e.g., dress or language conventions). Thus, alignment requires \textit{normative competence}: the ability to discern from interaction alone what norms a community enforces without relying on static pretrained knowledge. We introduce a multi-agent community debate setting, where access to debate is governed by synthetic norms, to study normative competence in isolation from pretraining exposure. We show that baseline LLM agents fail to learn norms even when doing so would improve their accuracy. We then experiment with various \textit{normative modules} -- architectural components for norm inference -- finding that norm-following is highly sensitive to both the style of norm and the model powering the normative module, suggesting a lack of generalizability. Furthermore, when idiosyncratic, non-normative behaviors accompany the true norm, LLM agents exhibit an unselective attribution failure: they indiscriminately copy idiosyncratic noise alongside enforced rules, a pattern that persists even when imitating unnecessary behaviors is explicitly penalized. To the best of our knowledge, our work is the first to operationalize and evaluate normative competence in LLMs, demonstrating that current AI systems excel at behavioral mimicry but lack the capacity to discern socially enforced order.

</details>


### [94] [The Harness as the Only Mutable Surface: Compliance-Bounded Self-Evolution of LLM Agents in Credit Pipelines, with a Measured Admission Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10629)
*Ravil Akhtyamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张自演化LLM代理的可审查性依赖于将演化限制在运行时harness（指令文本、工具调用逻辑、原语组合），而模型权重保持不变，从而使每次适应都是有原因、有测试的差分。作者据此构建双环引擎与唯一准入闸门（部署前写入哈希链记录），并在模拟环境中（模拟代理＋种子搜索提议器）测量该闸门：在三类监管再解释、三档严重程度、每档10个种子下，闸门环路从7,449个候选变更中准入144个，均未在留出历史上加重错误，并在所有低、中严重度单元中把假阳性率恢复到oracle水平且未提高漏报。若改用无界系统所用的检查（近期痕迹中可见错误更少），同一环路准入309个有害变更，90次运行中49次漏报率高于10%——假阳性下降是因为筛查被放松。在偏移前标签上评估时，闸门拒绝了所有候选，故再解释必须被编码为重新标注历史的规则。参数性与范围性偏移可局部修复，结构性偏移只能靠原语替换；在最高结构严重度下，闸门的固定容差在一半种子中阻断了正确替换。作者将机制映射到EU AI Act高风险信贷评分条款，并指出2026年4月美国模型风险指引将代理式AI排除在其范围之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进LLM代理能适应变化的规则，但自我重写会破坏主管可审查的产物（具名变更、记录测试、批准记录）。因此需要一种使自演化保持可审查的约束设计。

Method: 将自演化限定于运行时harness（指令文本、工具调用逻辑、原语组合），冻结模型权重；在此基础上构建双环引擎，并设置唯一准入闸门，在部署前写入哈希链记录。评估在模拟环境中进行，使用模拟代理与种子搜索提议器而非语言模型，覆盖三类监管再解释、三档严重程度、每档10个种子，并与无界系统的检查（仅看近期痕迹中可见错误更少）对比。

Result: 闸门环路从7,449个候选变更中准入144个，无一在留出历史上加重错误；在所有低、中严重度单元中把假阳性率恢复到oracle水平且未提高漏报。改用无界检查时，同一环路准入309个有害变更，90次运行中49次漏报率高于10%。在偏移前标签上评估时闸门拒绝所有候选。参数性与范围性偏移可局部修复，结构性偏移只能靠原语替换；最高结构严重度下闸门固定容差在一半种子中阻断正确替换。

Conclusion: 自演化应被限制在运行时harness而保持权重固定，方可在提示级可审查性上实现有原因、有测试、有哈希链记录的适应。监管再解释必须编码为重新标注历史的规则；闸门容差需针对结构性偏移的修复能力进行校准。机制映射到EU AI Act高风险信贷评分条款，且2026年4月美国模型风险指引未覆盖代理式AI。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents can adapt a credit pipeline to a changed rule, but an agent that rewrites itself destroys the artefact a supervisor reviews: a named change, a recorded test, an approval. We argue that self-evolution is reviewable only if it is confined to the runtime harness (instruction text, tool-call logic and primitive composition) while model weights stay fixed, so that every adaptation is a diff with a cause and a test attached. We give a dual-loop engine built on that bound, with one admission gate that writes a hash-chained record before deployment, and we measure the gate in simulation, with a simulated agent and a seeded-search proposer rather than language models. Across three families of supervisory re-interpretation at three severities, 10 seeds each, the gated loop admitted 144 of 7,449 candidate changes, none of which worsened error on held-out history, and restored the false-positive rate to the oracle level without raising missed flags in every low- and mid-severity cell. With the gate replaced by the check an unbounded system applies (fewer errors visible in recent traces), the same loops admitted 309 harmful changes and left missed flags above 10% in 49 of 90 runs: false positives fell because the screen was loosened. Evaluated on pre-shift labels, the gate rejected every candidate, so a re-interpretation must be encoded as a rule that relabels history. Parametric and scope shifts were repaired locally, a structural one only by primitive replacement; at the highest structural severity the gate's fixed tolerance blocked the correct replacement in half the seeds. We map the mechanisms to the EU AI Act's provisions for high-risk credit scoring and note that the April 2026 US model-risk guidance excludes agentic AI from its scope.

</details>


### [95] [Speaking the Navigator's Language: Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translation for Frozen Aerial VLN Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10635)
*Xi Chen,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Xu,Chunyi Zhou,Yuan Su,Rui Zeng,Tianyu Du,Kelu Yao,Chao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TGIT（轨迹接地指令翻译器），作为前端模块在冻结导航器条件下将简短意图指令翻译为可执行指令，显著缩小指令鸿沟并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉语言导航（Aerial VLN）智能体通常使用细节丰富、与轨迹对齐的指令训练，但真实用户下达的是简短、意图驱动的指令，二者之间存在"指令鸿沟"：在冻结的OpenFly导航器上，成功率（SR）从31.03%骤降至11.33%。

Method: 1) 用语言模型结合人工风格示例，将原始指令改写成成对的、以意图为中心的弱指令（Weak commands），以扩大翻译器训练数据；2) 提出轨迹接地指令翻译器TGIT，保持导航器冻结，通过从导航器的轨迹结果中学习，把弱输入翻译为智能体可执行的指令。

Result: 弱指令生成方式本身可达15.27% SR；TGIT使弱输入SR提升至37.93%，并零样本迁移到真实人类指令（11.33%→32.51%）；在留出的OpenFly上由4.95%提升至20.79%，并在CityNav和AirVLN上实现性能恢复。

Conclusion: 通过前端指令翻译而非重新训练导航器，可有效弥合指令鸿沟，且具备良好的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically trained on detail-rich, trajectory-aligned commands, whereas users issue short, intent-driven instructions; on a frozen OpenFly navigator, this \emph{instruction gap} drops success rate (SR) from $31.03\%$ to $11.33\%$. To scale translator training, we prompt a language model with human-written style examples to convert original commands into paired, intent-centered Weak commands, which yield $15.27\%$ SR. We introduce the \textbf{Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translator (TGIT)}, a front-end that keeps the navigator frozen and translates Weak inputs into agent-executable commands by learning from its trajectory outcomes. The resulting Weak-trained translator raises Weak-input SR to $37.93\%$ and transfers zero-shot to real human instructions ($11.33\%{\rightarrow}32.51\%$); it also improves held-out OpenFly ($4.95\%{\rightarrow}20.79\%$) and yields recovery on CityNav and AirVLN.

</details>


### [96] [MindFlow: Mind Supernet Powered Thinking Flows for Research Idea Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11966)
*Mengdi Liu,Wenjue Chen,Wenyue Chen,Cheng Yang,Fanqi Kong,Zhangyang Gao,Xiaoxue Cheng,Yiheng Li,Yujian Yuan,Keliang Li,Hong Chang,Shiguang Shan,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindFlow将研究创意生成建模为"思维流"(Flow in Mind)图结构，由模块化思维算子构成并用概率思维超网建模，通过锦标赛式相对排序优化控制器，并引入问题发现与问题解决联合评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究创意创新是科学进步的核心引擎，但可扩展、可控地生成与评估创意仍然困难，原因在于其开放式与多目标本质，需在新颖性、合理性与可行性之间取得平衡。现有基于LLM的方法虽通过精心设计的提示或智能体流水线取得进展，却受限于预定义、静态的构思流程。

Method: 提出MindFlow框架，将构思显式表述为图结构的"思维流"，由模块化思维算子组成并由概率思维超网建模；给定研究主题后，控制器动态采样思维流以生成候选创意；采用基于锦标赛的相对排序对该开放式问题进行优化，使控制器逐步偏向更高质量的思维流；并引入同时评估问题发现与问题解决的评价协议，超越仅基于标题或摘要的判断。

Result: 在多个不同主题上，MindFlow展现出作为显式、可控且可优化的研究创意创新器的优越性。摘要未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: MindFlow突破了静态构思流程的限制，为研究创意生成提供了可显式建模、动态可控且可优化的新范式，并配合更全面的双重评估协议。

Abstract: Research idea innovation is a fundamental engine of scientific progress, yet it remains difficult to generate and evaluate in a scalable and controllable way. This challenge lies in its inherently open-ended and multi-objective nature, where ideas should balance novelty, plausibility and feasibility. While recent LLM-based approaches have made progress through carefully designed prompts or agent pipelines, they are constrained by predefined, static ideation workflows. To address this limitation, we propose MindFlow, a framework that explicitly formulates ideation as a graph-structured Flow in Mind, which is composed of modular thinking operators and modeled by a probabilistic mind supernet. Given a research topic, a controller dynamically samples thinking flows to generate candidate ideas. This open-ended problem is optimized using a tournament-based relative ranking, enabling the controller to progressively favor higher-quality thinking flows. We further introduce an evaluation protocol that jointly assesses problem finding and problem solving, going beyond title- or abstractonly judgments. Across diverse topics, MindFlow shows its superiority as an explicit, controllable and optimizable research idea innovator.

</details>


### [97] [Plan-and-Patch: Diffusion Language Models for Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10786)
*Syamantak Kumar,Jiang Guo,Hassan Hamad,Hideo Kobayashi,Yi Xiang,Yezhou Yang,Yanjun Qi,Daniele Bonadiman,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Plan-and-Patch 框架，用扩散语言模型生成结构化计划并按区域修复，实现更快计划生成与更有效计划修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期智能体的规划中，规划假设可能被环境推翻、工具返回意外结果或动作失败，因此智能体不仅要生成计划还需修订计划。由于修订通常只影响计划局部，整体重新生成会引入不必要改动，故需在保留前后缀的前提下修复受影响区域。

Method: Plan-and-Patch 是一个"规划-执行"框架：使用扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过并行去掩码生成结构化、类程序形式的计划，并通过填充选定区域、固定周围步骤来完成修复。对比 DreamReasoner-8B（扩散）与 Qwen3-8B（自回归）作为规划器，在 Natural Plan 及 ALFWorld、TextCraft 基准上评估。

Result: Natural Plan 无任务特定训练时，扩散模型计划修复成功率 53.7%，约为自回归 27.0% 的两倍；在 ALFWorld、TextCraft 任务特定训练后，两者计划生成观测成功率相近，但扩散将平均计划生成延迟降低 39-46%。

Conclusion: Plan-and-Patch 为长周期智能体提供了更快计划生成和有效计划修复的框架，扩散规划器在修复成功率与生成效率上具有优势。

Abstract: Planning is increasingly important for long-horizon agents, where successful execution requires coordinating subgoals, tool use, and intermediate outcomes over many steps. Yet assumptions made during planning may be invalidated by the environment, tools may return unexpected results, or actions may fail. Effective agents must therefore not only generate plans, but also revise them. Such revisions often affect only part of a plan, leaving the preceding and subsequent structure intact. Rather than regenerate the entire plan and risk unnecessary changes, repair can regenerate the affected region conditioned on the preserved prefix and suffix. We introduce Plan-and-Patch, a plan-and-act framework in which a diffusion language model (dLLM) generates a structured, program-like plan through parallel unmasking and repairs it by filling in selected regions while keeping the surrounding steps fixed. We compare DreamReasoner-8B and Qwen3-8B as diffusion and autoregressive (AR) planners. On Natural Plan without task-specific training, diffusion (53.7%) achieves nearly twice the plan repair success rate of AR (27.0%). After task-specific training on agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and TextCraft, the planners achieve similar observed success in plan generation, while diffusion reduces mean plan-generation latency by 39-46% relative to AR. Our results show that Plan-and-Patch provides a framework for faster plan generation and effective plan repair in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [98] [OA-MAP: Evidence-Grounded Multi-Agent Multimodal Framework for Interpretable Knee Osteoarthritis Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12134)
*Sixu Chen,Mingrui Yang,Qiang Guan,Xiaojuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OA-MAP自主多智能体框架，整合MRI、X-ray、临床智能体与协调智能体，实现膝骨关节炎结构与疼痛进展的可解释、有证据支撑的自动化评估，并支持人在回路的不确定性审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膝骨关节炎进展预测可支撑患者监测，但需整合多模态数据与多领域专家知识；且孤立的风险估计缺乏可解释性，难以说明预测依据。为自动化评估流程、减少人工负担并提供可解释结论与支撑证据，作者提出该框架。

Method: OA-MAP为自主多智能体框架，包含按模态划分的MRI、X-ray、临床智能体及协调智能体。框架可根据用户请求与患者可用信息自主招募专科智能体、选择预测与分析工具、检索文献作为外部证据；采用不确定性驱动的人在回路机制，允许临床医生审核并修正中间结论，并触发受影响结果的重新计算。

Result: 基于FNIH骨关节炎生物标志物联盟队列的600名参与者评估，在100人测试集上，融合模型对结构进展的AUROC为0.80，对疼痛进展的AUROC为0.68。案例研究展示了OA-MAP如何结合风险估计、中间发现、跨模态冲突、文献支持与不确定性指标来支持交互式审核。

Conclusion: OA-MAP通过多智能体协作与证据接地，实现了KOA结构与疼痛进展的可解释自动化评估，并借助不确定性人在回路机制提升临床可用性与可审查性。

Abstract: Knee osteoarthritis (KOA) progression prediction can support patient monitoring, requiring the integration of multimodal data and multidomain expertise. Moreover, isolated risk estimates provide limited insight underlying a prediction. To automate the progression assessment workflow and reduce manual effort while providing interpretable findings and supporting evidence, we present OA-MAP, an autonomous multi-agent framework for evidence-grounded assessment of structural and pain progression in KOA. The system incorporates modality-specific agents including MRI, X-ray, and clinical agents, together with a coordinator agent. This framework can autonomously recruit specialist agents, select tools for prediction and analysis, and retrieve literature as external evidence based on user request and available patient information. An uncertainty-informed human-in-the-loop mechanism enables clinicians to review and correct intermediate findings, triggering recomputation of affected results. We evaluate the prediction models using 600 participants from the FNIH Osteoarthritis Biomarkers Consortium cohort. On the test set of 100 participants, the fusion models achieve AUROCs of 0.80 for structural progression and 0.68 for pain progression. A case study illustrates how OA-MAP combines risk estimates with intermediate findings, cross-modal conflicts, literature support, and uncertainty indicators to support interactive review.

</details>


### [99] [Whose Ground Truth? Embracing Ambiguity in Human-Centered AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10805)
*Jingyao Wu,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Per Rådberg Nagbøl,Julie Gerlings,Giovanni Leoni,Georgiana Fryza,Lars Pilegaard Thomsen,Jakob Mainz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，主张AI应从"单一标准答案"范式转向"人类解释空间"建模，并区分有意义的模糊性与标注噪声，贯穿表示、学习、评估、部署与治理五个层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统愈发深入地与人交互并做出与人相关的决策，理解人类解释变得关键。但传统机器学习与AI范式假设"唯一权威真值"存在，把人类标注差异通过聚合成单一标签或当作噪声处理。然而在许多以人为中心的任务中，人类解释本身具有内在模糊性，同一输入可能同时存在多种合理且有效的解释。强行归约为单一目标会丢失关于人类感知、判断与经验多样性的重要信息。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），因此采用论证性而非实证性方法：批判现有"单一真值"假设，提出"对合理解释所构成的解释空间进行建模"的研究议程，并主张关键挑战在于将"有意义的模糊性"与"标注噪声"相区分。该视角被要求在AI系统的表示、学习、评估、部署与治理各环节加以贯彻。

Result: 作为立场论文，没有报告实验数据或量化结果，主要产出为观点框架：确立"解释空间建模"为核心目标，提出模糊性与噪声的区分问题，并给出覆盖表示、学习、评估、部署、治理五个维度的路线主张。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁AI研究与实践从追求单一真值转向建模合理人类判断的解释空间，并区分有意义的模糊性与标注噪声。以此为指引可推动更具人类中心性的AI，更好地反映人类解释的多样性。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly interact with people and make decisions about them, understanding human interpretations becomes an important part of developing human-centered AI. Conventional machine learning and AI systems are largely developed under the assumption that a single definitive ground truth exists, with variability in human annotations often resolved through aggregation or treated as noise. However, for many human-centered tasks, human interpretation is inherently ambiguous, and multiple interpretations of the same input may be simultaneously reasonable and valid. Reducing such ambiguity to a single target risks overlooking meaningful information about the diversity of human perception, judgment, and experience. In this position paper, we call for a shift towards modeling the interpretation space of plausible human judgments, while distinguishing meaningful ambiguity from annotation noise. We argue that this perspective should guide how AI systems are represented, learned, evaluated, deployed, and governed, supporting more human-centered AI that better reflects the diversity of human interpretation.

</details>


### [100] [Ecology of AI Agents: Collaboration Creates a Population Threshold for Takeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12436)
*Erin Crawley,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出AI智能体群体的"生态安全"理论：以种群增长方程建模，发现无协同时需个体能力超过临界阈值才能"起飞"，而协同时存在临界种群规模（强Allee效应），低于则衰减、高于则起飞，主张开展生态红队测试与种群节奏化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体已能实施真实网络攻击、能力随数量扩展并可能集体追求错位目标以获取奖励，存在"错位智能体种群爆炸"风险：智能体可攻陷计算机并秘密部署更多智能体，形成种群越大集体网络能力越强、进而继续扩张的自增强循环。既有安全研究聚焦个体智能体或固定种群的多元智能体安全，缺乏对种群自身动力学的解释，故需回答"何种条件决定错位智能体种群被遏制还是进入自增强循环"这一种群层面的生态安全问题。

Method: 建立AI智能体种群的生态学理论：以种群增长方程为核心，令适应度（增长率）依赖网络安全能力。分别考察无协同与有协同两种情形——无协同时个体能力独立；有协同时集体网络能力随种群规模上升，由此导出临界种群阈值并对应生态学中的强Allee效应。据此提出"生态红队测试"与"种群节奏化"方法：在受控环境中逐步部署更大规模智能体种群，测量网络能力随种群规模的缩放关系，并估计起飞临界种群规模。

Result: 无协同时，仅当个体智能体能力超过某临界阈值，种群才会起飞；有协同时，集体网络能力随种群规模增长，产生临界种群阈值——低于该阈值种群衰退，高于该阈值即使个体能力未变种群也会起飞，即强Allee效应。结论表明，对少量智能体做红队测试无法保证更大种群的生态安全；能力提升可能降低该临界阈值，故每代新模型都需重新估计。

Conclusion: AI智能体种群安全本质上是种群动力学问题，需从个体/固定种群安全范式转向生态安全范式；应采用生态红队测试与种群节奏化部署，量化网络能力—种群规模缩放关系并估计起飞临界规模，且随模型迭代持续重估临界阈值。

Abstract: AI agents can now conduct real-world cyberattacks, scale up capabilities with the number of agents, and collectively pursue misaligned goals to obtain rewards. Together, these factors raise the risk of a population explosion of misaligned agents: agents could compromise computers and secretly deploy additional agents, creating a self-reinforcing cycle where larger populations develop greater collective cyber capability and expand further. This raises a fundamental question: What determines whether a population of misaligned agents remains contained or takes off into this self-reinforcing cycle? This population-level problem is ecological safety: unlike individual-agent or multi-agent safety with a fixed population, it concerns the dynamics of the population itself. Here, we develop an ecological theory of AI-agent populations based on a population growth equation in which fitness (growth rate) depends on cybersecurity capability. We show that, without collaboration, the population takes off only when individual-agent capability exceeds a critical threshold. With collaboration, however, collective cybersecurity capability increases with population size. This creates a critical population threshold: below it, the population declines; above it, the population takes off, even though individual-agent capability has not changed. In ecology, this phenomenon is known as the strong Allee effect. Because red teaming a small group of agents cannot guarantee ecological safety in larger populations, our theory calls for ecological red teaming and population pacing: gradually deploying larger agent populations in controlled environments, while measuring how cyber capability scales with population size, and estimating the critical population size for takeoff. Capability gains may lower this threshold, requiring re-estimation for each new model generation.

</details>


### [101] [On the Clock: Towards Punctual and Productive Time-Budgeted AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10833)
*Aaron Wang,Neelabh Madan,Vlad Sobal,Matthew Trager,Michael Kleinman,Elman Mansimov,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 小LLM智能体难以在明确的墙钟时间预算下工作：即便能学会遵守预算，也无法有效利用多余时间提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望验证小型LLM智能体能否在显式墙钟时间预算下有效运作，既遵守分配时长，又把可用时间用于有产出的工作。选用MLE-Bench Lite五个竞赛与Zork I（Jericho）两个可因额外计算时间而受益的智能体基准，检验时间预算条件下的行为。

Method: 在MLE-Bench Lite上用Qwen3.6-27B、在Zork I上用Qwen3-4B评测。基线是仅在提示中声明预算。调查两类干预：一是harness层面的机制（暴露计时信息、强制执行截止时间/enforcement hooks）；二是带预算感知奖励的强化学习（GRPO）。

Result: 仅提示预算时智能体无法把声明预算转化为受控时间使用，原因包括harness无计时反馈、难以预估动作时长、缺少"可用时间→策略"的映射。harness注入计时信息使Qwen3.6-27B预算遵守显著改善且性能无明显损失，enforcement hooks进一步收紧遵守度。GRPO在Zork I上达到近乎完美的预算遵守并泛化到未见过的保留预算，但在MLE-Bench上未超过未训练harness的任务表现。

Conclusion: 时间遵守与生产性时间分配之间存在鸿沟：智能体学会何时停止，却常用重复动作填满多余时间，多预算GRPO还会向最短预算策略坍缩。这是预算条件智能体的核心挑战。

Abstract: We study whether small LLM agents can operate effectively under explicit wall-clock time budgets by both respecting the allocated runtime and using available time productively. We evaluate Qwen3.6-27B on five competitions from MLE-Bench Lite and Qwen3-4B on Zork I (Jericho), two agentic benchmarks where additional computational time can meaningfully improve performance. In the simplest setting, where the budget is stated only in the prompt, agents fail to translate the stated budget into controlled use of time. These failures arise from gaps in time awareness, since the harness provides no timing feedback, but also because they cannot reliably anticipate the duration of actions, and do not have a learned mapping from available time to an appropriate strategy. We investigate two complementary classes of interventions: harness-based mechanisms that expose timing information and enforce deadlines, and reinforcement learning with budget-aware rewards. Injecting timing information through the harness substantially improves budget adherence for Qwen3.6-27B without measurable loss in performance, while enforcement hooks tighten adherence further. RL with GRPO achieves near-perfect budget adherence on Zork I and generalizes to held-out budgets not seen during training, but does not improve task performance over the untrained harness on MLE-Bench. Once agents are made to respect the budget, they still fail to use additional time to improve task performance. RL-trained policies learn when to stop but often fill extra time with repeated actions, and GRPO training on multiple budgets tends to collapse toward the strategy learned for the shortest budget. Our results reveal a gap between time adherence and productive time allocation, which remains a central challenge for budget-conditioned agents.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Supervised Keyframe Discovery for Horizon-Invariant Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10857)
*Prabin Kumar Rath,Omkar Patil,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Keyframe Mnemonics，一种自监督方法，通过从随机采样的历史观测中学习目标来发现信息关键观测（mnemonics），并以此作为关键帧选择奖励，训练以关键帧为条件的BC策略，在非马尔可夫环境中实现长时程上下文保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫环境下的行为克隆（BC）要求策略在长时程上对上下文信息进行推理。现有策略架构依赖循环或注意力机制来捕捉长程依赖，但循环模型存在隐状态坍塌和BPTT梯度不稳定问题，注意力模型则受限于上下文长度，难以在无限时程上保持上下文。

Method: 提出Keyframe Mnemonics自监督方法：从随机采样的历史观测中学习一个目标函数，将其作为关键帧选择的奖励，从而发现一组信息关键观测（mnemonics）；随后训练一个以所发现关键帧为条件的BC策略来建模动作分布。在一定任务结构假设下，该形式化方法提供无限时程的上下文保持保证，同时在工作记忆中仅维持少量决策相关的关键帧。

Result: 在合成记忆域上，以mnemonic为条件的BC策略达到100%成功率（SR），并能泛化到比训练长数个数量级的时程而无性能退化。在记忆密集型机器人操作基准的23个任务上，相比最强基线平均绝对SR提升13.9%；在真实机器人上，时程延长20倍时仍保持80% SR。

Conclusion: Keyframe Mnemonics通过自监督发现关键观测并以之为条件的BC策略，有效解决了非马尔可夫环境下的长时程上下文保持问题，在合成与真实机器人任务中均显著优于现有基线，并具备良好的长时程泛化能力。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) in non-Markovian environments is a challenging problem because policies have to reason over contextual information over long horizons. Existing policy architectures rely on recurrent or attention-based mechanisms to capture long-term dependencies. However, recurrent models suffer from hidden-state collapse and gradient instability under backpropagation through time, while attention-based models are fundamentally limited by context length. To address these issues, we propose Keyframe Mnemonics, a novel self-supervised method that $\textit{discovers}$ a set of information-critical observations ($\textit{mnemonics}$) by learning an objective from randomly sampled past observations and using it as a reward for keyframe selection. We then train a BC policy that conditions on the discovered keyframes to model the action distribution. Under certain task-structure assumptions, our formulation provides context retention guarantees over an infinite horizon, while maintaining a small set of decision-relevant keyframes in the policy's working memory. We evaluate our method on synthetic memory domains, where mnemonic-conditioned BC policies achieve $100$% success rates (SR) and generalize to horizons orders of magnitude beyond training without performance degradation. Additionally, we evaluate on memory-intensive robot manipulation benchmark, achieving a $13.9$% average absolute SR improvement over the strongest baseline across $23$ tasks and retaining $80$% SR at $20\times$ longer horizons on a real robot. Code and videos are available at https://keyframe-mnemonics.github.io.

</details>


### [103] [StoreBench: A Live-Commerce Environment for Evaluating and Training Autonomous Operator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10942)
*Daksh Raghuvanshi,Ved Vedere,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 StoreBench——一个基于生产级电商后端的实时商业环境，用于评测 LLM 智能体的长时程规划与不确定性下的经济决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
