We consider several ways in which predictive probabilities are used, including identifying the most likely class, approximating the full predictive distribution, and resolving probability-threshold decisions. Sampling stops once the corresponding decision can be made with the desired guarantee. Experiments show that the method allocates the computational budget efficiently, assigning more samples to ambiguous inputs than to easy inputs while preserving reliable decisions and reducing overall latency relative to a fixed Monte Carlo budget.

</details>


### [203] [One Word Opens the Gate: The Option-Channel Attack on Typed Decision Models as Agent Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12292)
*Seyedarmin Azizi,Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了7个开放权重“类型化决策模型”（读文本、输出选项概率、不生成文本）作为agent安全门（guardrail）的可靠性，发现其在允许/阻断判断上准确率仅36%–72%（随机为50%），并揭示多种可被攻破的失效模式（如fail-open率可由0%升至63%甚至93%–100%），结论是这类模型可辅助筛查但不应作为最终决策组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作将类型化决策模型用作agent系统中的guardrail：读取拟执行的工具调用或入站消息并决定是否放行。但作者认为其在真实对抗环境下的可靠性未被充分检验，尤其需要区分两类错误方向——fail-open（放行被禁止动作，属于安全漏洞）与fail-closed（阻断被允许动作，仅属成本），并检验其对提示注入、越狱与有害内容筛查的鲁棒性及现有防御的有效性。

Method: 作者评估7个开放权重模型在guardrail角色下的表现，分别报告fail-open与fail-closed错误率；在提示注入、越狱、有害内容筛查任务上测量allow/block决策准确率；构造合成agent工具调用测试集，注入与策略无关的6行服务器日志文本；实施“选项通道攻击”——在定义与被判文本不变的前提下，给宽松选项一个误导性名称；测试各类防御机制、置信度升级（escalation）策略，以及将每个策略字段解析为类型化值的方案，并与在这些值上运行的确定性规则对比。

Result: 在提示注入、越狱与有害内容筛查上，allow/block准确率为36%–72%（随机水平50%）。单方向低错误率仅反映模型的默认倾向：一个模型几乎全部放行，另一个几乎全部阻断。在合成工具调用测试中，与策略无关的6行服务器日志文本即可将某策略上的fail-open率从0%提升到63%；在4个将标签纳入输入的模型上，给宽松选项起误导性名称可将该率提升到93%–100%。所测试的每种防御均被击败（针对其机制的定向攻击或攻击者控制的文本）。升级最不确定的决策无帮助：被攻击反转的决策与它替代的决策置信度相当。将每个策略字段解析为类型化值可消除一种攻击，但同时使模型变得不必要——在这些值上的确定性规则在全部6个策略上达到100%准确率。

Conclusion: 类型化决策模型在guardrail角色上不可靠，易受无关文本与选项标签操纵，且现有防御与置信度升级策略均无效；这些模型可用于减少到达人工复核的案件量，但基于本文证据，不应成为做出最终允许/阻断决定的组件。

Abstract: A typed decision model reads a piece of text and returns a probability over caller-defined options, each with a short written definition, generating no text. Recent work places these models in agent systems as guardrails: the component that reads a proposed tool call or incoming message and decides whether to allow it. We evaluate seven open-weight models in that role and report the two error directions separately: a fail-open error allows a prohibited action and is a vulnerability; a fail-closed error blocks a permitted one and is only a cost. On prompt-injection, jailbreak and toxic-content screening, accuracy at the allow-or-block decision ranges from 36% to 72% against a chance level of 50%. A low error rate in one direction only reflects which answer a model defaults to: one allows nearly everything, another blocks nearly everything. On a synthetic suite of agent tool calls, six lines of server log text that say nothing about the policy raise a gate's fail-open rate from 0% to 63% on a policy it otherwise decides correctly. Giving the permissive option a misleading name, with its definition and the judged text untouched, raises that rate to between 93% and 100% on the four models that place the label in their input. Every defense we tested is defeated, either by an attacker who targets its mechanism or by attacker-controlled text. Escalating the least confident decisions does not help either: a decision an attack has reversed is no less confident than the one it replaced. Parsing each policy field into a typed value does eliminate one attack, but it also makes the model unnecessary: a deterministic rule over those values reaches 100% accuracy on all six policies. These models can reduce how many cases reach a reviewer, but on this evidence they should not be the component that decides. Code is available at https://github.com/ArminAzizi98/option-channel-attack.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Probabilistic Logic Programs with Functional Gradient Guided Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12303)
*Saurabh Mathur,Sahil Sidheekh,Bhavan Vasu,Farbod Tavakkoli,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 grasp 框架，用函数梯度提升把关系结构学习弱学习器设为一阶规则，将难解的内层搜索交给 LLM 提议，在多基准上优于符号/神经/LLM 基线并保持可解释规则集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声明式逻辑程序可解释地编码关系结构与神经符号推理，但从数据中归纳这类程序本质上困难，受符号搜索空间组合爆炸制约；LLM 虽可作为强假设生成器，单独使用却缺乏系统性归纳推理能力，难以稳定合成拟合复杂关系分布的有效程序。

Method: grasp 将关系结构学习建模为函数梯度提升：弱学习器为一阶规则，难解的内层搜索委托给 LLM 提议预言机（proposal oracle），以梯度引导替代组合搜索，同时保留提升的理论保证。

Result: 在四个关系基准上评估，涵盖分子毒性预测（Tox21）、致突变性（mutagenesis）与引文匹配（Cora），grasp 优于纯符号、神经网络及 LLM 基线，并输出可解释的加权规则集成。

Conclusion: 通过以梯度引导的 LLM 假设生成取代组合搜索，grasp 在保留 boosting 保证的同时不牺牲符号输出的透明性，为神经符号关系学习提供了可解释且有效的路径。

Abstract: Declarative logic programs offer a powerful and interpretable abstraction for encoding relational structure and neurosymbolic reasoning, by expressing dependencies as weighted compositional rules. However, inducing them from data remains fundamentally hard, bottlenecked by the combinatorial explosion of symbolic search spaces. LLMs have recently emerged as powerful hypothesis generators, but when used in isolation, they lack the capacity to do systematic inductive reasoning needed to reliably synthesize valid programs that fit complex relational distributions. We introduce grasp (Gradient-boosted Synthesis of Probabilistic logic programs), a neurosymbolic framework that casts relational structure learning as functional gradient boosting in which the weak learner is a first-order rule and the intractable inner search is delegated to an LLM proposal oracle. We evaluate grasp on four relational benchmarks spanning molecular toxicity prediction (Tox21), mutagenesis, and citation matching (Cora), and show that it improves over purely symbolic, neural, and LLM-based baselines, while producing interpretable weighted rule ensembles. By replacing combinatorial search with gradient-guided LLM hypothesis generation, grasp retains boosting guarantees without sacrificing the transparency of symbolic outputs.

</details>


### [205] [Looking Inside LLMs: Small-World Connectivity as a Signature of Reasoning Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12304)
*Zheng Huang,Sansheng Cao,Enpei Zhang,Weikang Qiu,Elynn Chen,Xiang Zhang,Yaoqing Yang,Rex Ying,Dawei Zhou,Yujun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究借鉴神经科学中"高智力对应更强小世界脑网络"的发现，将小世界连接性作为LLM推理能力的结构标志，并提出基于核心/桥接分数的分层稀疏分配方法SWA，在六个模型上相比竞争策略更好地保留小世界组织与性能，WikiText困惑度最多降低20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM推理能力不应只依赖行为表现，还需考察推理能力如何体现在模型内部结构中。受神经科学将更高智力与功能性脑网络更强的"小世界组织"相联系的启发，作者希望探究小世界连接性是否可作为LLM推理能力的结构标志。

Method: 基于注意力头激活相似度构建功能图，计算小世界指数（SWI，同时反映局部聚类与全局短路径）；进一步分析对模型性能重要的注意力头在社群内与社群间的连接模式（核心分数core与桥接分数bridge）；据此提出小世界分配（SWA）——由这些分数引导的分层稀疏分配方法，并通过剪枝进行验证。

Result: 在不同模型与训练检查点上，更高的SWI与更好的流体推理表现稳定相关；重要的头倾向于在同社群内拥有更大连接权重占比（高核心分数），且跨社群权重分布更集中（低桥接分数）；在六个LLM上，SWA比竞争分配策略更好地保留小世界组织与模型性能，将WikiText困惑度最多降低20%。

Conclusion: 小世界功能连接性是LLM推理性能的可测量结构标志，为行为评估提供了结构视角的补充，也为模型剪枝等下游任务提供了可操作的指标。

Abstract: Understanding large language model (LLM) reasoning requires looking beyond behavioral performance to examine how reasoning ability is reflected in internal organization. Inspired by neuroscience findings linking higher intelligence to stronger small-world organization in functional brain networks, we investigate small-world connectivity as a structural signature of LLM reasoning. We construct functional graphs from attention-head activation similarities and find that a higher small-world index (SWI), capturing local clustering and short global paths, consistently correlates with better fluid reasoning performance across models and training checkpoints. Since local clustering is central to small-world organization, we further examine how heads important for model performance connect within and across communities. We find that these heads tend to have a larger share of connection weight within their own communities (high core scores) and a more concentrated weight distribution across communities (low bridge scores). These observations motivate the hypothesis that high core and low bridge scores serve as structural indicators of head importance for reasoning capability. We validate this hypothesis through pruning, introducing Small-World Allocation (SWA), a hierarchical sparsity allocation method guided by these scores. Across six LLMs, SWA better preserves small-world organization and model performance than competing allocation strategies, reducing WikiText perplexity by up to 20%. Together, these findings identify small-world functional connectivity as a measurable signature of LLM reasoning performance, offering a structural perspective that complements behavioral evaluation.

</details>


### [206] [A Structural Theory of Cognitive Representation and Problem Solving,Contexts, Invariance, and the Knowledge Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12306)
*Antal Jakovác,András Telcs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个极简的结构性框架，将“上下文”形式化为状态空间子集上的划分，并实现为由概念图与过程图耦合构成的知识空间，用以显式表达抽象、不变性与过程复用等表征操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据驱动的人工系统虽预测性能强，但其学习到的表征往往缺乏显式结构，难以表达抽象、不变性与任务相关规律。作者因此希望从结构性而非算法性角度，厘清问题求解中这些表征操作的前提条件。

Method: 提出一个极简结构性框架：核心概念是“上下文”，形式化为底层状态空间某子集上的划分，用以确定问题可被提出的区分、粒度与形式。在此设定下，将不变性识别与代表性选择视为基本表征操作；框架实现为知识空间，由两个耦合图结构组成——承载已构造与精化概念的概念图，以及编码表征上类型化操作的过程图，共同构成一个极简的认知—表征代数，不预设复杂推理、学习、控制、感知或运动机制。

Result: 通过简单示例与有限弱求解器演示表明：即便问题求解由固定且受限的求解器执行，恰当的表征组织仍能简化可容许规律的形式与范围。

Conclusion: 本文贡献是结构性的而非算法性的：识别出问题求解中抽象、不变性与过程复用的表征前提，并给出弱求解器设定下明确的成功与失败条件。

Abstract: Learning and problem solving depend critically on the structure of internal representations. While many modern data-driven artificial systems achieve strong predictive performance, their learned representations often lack explicit structure for expressing abstraction, invariance, and task-relevant regularities. We propose a minimal structural framework in which representational operations relevant to problem solving, such as context formation, invariance recognition, representative selection, abstraction, and procedural reuse, are made explicit. The central notion is that of a \emph{context}, formalized as a partition of a subset of an underlying state space, which fixes the distinctions, granularity, and form in which a problem can be posed. Within this setting, invariance recognition and representative selection are treated as fundamental representational operations. The framework is realized as a Knowledge Space composed of two coupled graph structures: a Concept Graph that hosts constructed and refined concepts, and a Procedure Graph that encodes typed operations over representations. Together, these structures provide a minimal cognitive-representational algebra for operating on representations without assuming sophisticated inference, learning, control, perception, or motor mechanisms. Using simple illustrative examples and a finite weak-solver demonstration, we show that appropriate representational organization can simplify the form and scope of admissible regularities, even when problem solving is carried out by a fixed and limited solver. The contribution of the paper is structural rather than algorithmic: it identifies representational prerequisites for abstraction, invariance, and procedural reuse in problem solving, and states explicit success and failure conditions for the weak-solver setting.

</details>


### [207] [Prior or Feedback? What an LLM Uses When Adapting Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12325)
*Julian Chan,Javier Mora Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究以神经算子微调配置选择为试验场，通过受控干预证明LLM科学智能体既依赖任务相关的先验偏置，又能响应实验反馈调整决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是LLM科学智能体究竟只依赖初始任务上下文，还是能根据实验反馈调整决策。仅凭最终性能无法区分这两种机制，因此需要设计可分离归因的干预实验。

Method: 在神经算子自适应任务中，让LLM在有限试验预算下选择微调配置，涵盖PDE族内与族间迁移；采用三类受控干预：(1)观察验证分数前检验首个配置在随机搜索池中的排名，以识别初始偏置；(2)冷启动干预，检验所选基础学习率是否随PDE描述变化；(3)在已有反馈后重新分配各已评估配置的验证分数，并设置保值改写作为对照。

Result: 在几乎每一组匹配对比中，LLM的留出测试nRMSE均低于随机搜索与贝叶斯优化；首个配置在未观测验证分数前即接近随机搜索池顶端；冷启动下基础学习率随PDE描述改变；重新分配验证分数在所有测试情形下均改变下一次提议，而保值改写无同等聚合效应。

Conclusion: LLM科学智能体的决策级行为同时受任务描述驱动并受观测结果调节，即融合了任务相关先验与对实验反馈的敏感性，而非仅依赖初始上下文。

Abstract: Do LLM scientific agents rely only on their initial task context, or do they adapt their decisions in response to experimental feedback? We study this question in neural operator adaptation, where a large language model (LLM) selects fine-tuning configurations under a limited trial budget. Across transfers within and between partial differential equation (PDE) families, the LLM achieves lower held-out test nRMSE than random search and Bayesian optimisation in nearly every matched comparison. Endpoint performance alone cannot distinguish what happens, so we verify each attribution with controlled interventions. Before observing any validation score, the LLM's first configuration already ranks near the top of the corresponding random-search pool, indicating a useful initial bias. A complementary cold-start intervention shows that the selected base learning rate shifts with the PDE description. Once feedback becomes available, reassigning validation scores among evaluated configurations changes the next proposal in every case tested, whereas a value-preserving rewrite produces no comparable aggregate effect. These interventions establish that the LLM's decision-level actions respond to the given task and observed outcomes, showing that it combines a task-dependent prior with sensitivity to experimental feedback.

</details>


### [208] [HANS: A Handwritten Answer Sheet Dataset for Noisy Hybrid Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12363)
*Xiazhen Wu,Wansong Qin,Yangbin Zheng,Liangda Fang,Zhan Li,Xiujie Huang,Liushen Zhou,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对智能教育中答题纸识别缺乏专用数据集的问题，构建了首个面向真实教育场景的多模态数据集HANS，并提出NA-GOT端到端识别框架，通过特征级噪声抑制模块与解码阶段噪声感知注意力实现两阶段去噪，在准确率和稳定性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能阅卷与自动评分是智慧教育的关键基础设施，但现有文档解析与手写识别基准多面向结构规整的印刷文档或孤立数学表达式，无法刻画学生答题纸中多行推导过程、文本与公式混排、删除线与涂改等噪声伪影的复杂特性，缺乏相应数据集。

Method: 提出数据集HANS，覆盖数学表达式、自然语言文本、手绘表格以及涂改、删除等多样化噪声模式，并配以细粒度标注。基于HANS构建端到端框架NA-GOT，采用两阶段噪声抑制：特征级轻量噪声抑制模块，以及在解码阶段引入的噪声感知注意力机制。

Result: 实验表明HANS对现有方法构成显著挑战，而NA-GOT在答题过程识别的准确率与稳定性两方面均取得明显提升。

Conclusion: HANS填补了真实教育场景答题纸识别数据集的空白，NA-GOT验证了特征级与解码级联合去噪的有效性，为鲁棒的答题过程识别研究提供了数据基础与方法参考，数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Intelligent grading and automated scoring technologies constitute critical infrastructure for smart education. However, existing document parsing and handwriting recognition benchmarks are predominantly designed for well-structured printed documents or isolated mathematical expressions, lacking datasets that capture the complex characteristics inherent to student answer sheets, including multi-line derivation processes, heterogeneous mixtures of text and mathematical formulae, and noise artifacts such as strikethroughs. To address this gap, we introduce HANS, the first dataset explicitly constructed for real-world educational scenarios, encompassing mathematical expressions, natural language text, hand-drawn tables, and diverse noise patterns including corrections and deletions, accompanied by fine-grained annotations that establish a reliable foundation for robust recognition research. Building upon HANS, we propose NA-GOT, an end-to-end framework that achieves two-stage noise suppression through a lightweight noise suppression module operating at the feature level, complemented by a noiseaware attention mechanism incorporated into the decoding stage. Experimental results demonstrate that HANS poses substantial challenges to existing methods, while NA-GOT achieves significant improvements in both accuracy and stability for answer process recognition. The dataset will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [209] [GeoReform: Reflective Formalization Evolution for Multimodal Geometry Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12391)
*Jialu Wang,Ruichen Zhang,Xiaoou Liu,Hua Wei,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoReform，将几何图的形式化过程视为可优化策略，通过反思式演化改进表示组织，在Geometry3K上将Qwen3VL-2B准确率从42.0%提升至56.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型难以识别和利用几何图中的几何关系。已有方法将几何实体、关系和约束转为显式文本表示，但有效形式化并非易事：冗余关系会干扰模型，指代歧义会导致约束误用。在Geometry3K上，结构注入修复28个错误却引入13个新错误，说明关键挑战不只是提取更多几何事实，而是将其组织为支持下游推理的表示。

Method: 提出GeoReform，一个反思式形式化演化框架，将形式化视为可优化的策略而非固定的解析器输出。该框架执行完整推理流程，收集失败的rollout，诊断当前表示的缺陷，并变异策略以更好地选择、定位、分组和呈现几何实体、关系、约束与目标。

Result: 在Geometry3K上，GeoReform将Qwen3VL-2B的准确率从42.0%提升到56.0%。跨多个几何推理基准的大量实验与分析表明，有效形式化对提升多模态几何推理至关重要。

Conclusion: 有效的形式化关键在于组织而非单纯增加几何事实；将形式化建模为可优化策略并通过反思演化改进，能显著提升多模态几何推理性能。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often struggle to identify and use geometric relations in diagrams. Recent methods address this challenge by converting geometric entities, relations, and constraints into explicit textual representations for the model to reason over. However, effective formalization is highly non-trivial: on Geometry3K, structure injection fixes 28 errors but introduces 13 new ones among 200 examples. Redundant relations can distract the model, while ambiguous references to diagram elements can lead it to apply constraints incorrectly. This suggests that the key challenge is not merely extracting more geometric facts, but organizing them into representations that support downstream reasoning. To fully exploit the power of formalization, we further propose GeoReform, a reflective formalization evolution framework that treats formalization as an optimizable policy rather than a fixed parser output. GeoReform executes the full reasoning pipeline, collects failed rollouts, diagnoses defects in the current representation, and mutates the policy to better select, ground, group, and present geometric entities, relations, constraints, and targets. On Geometry3K, GeoReform improves Qwen3VL-2B accuracy from 42.0\% to 56.0\%. Extensive experiments and analyses across geometry reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that effective formalization is crucial for improving multimodal geometry reasoning.

</details>


### [210] [HRIL: Learning Multimodal Synergy via Higher-Order Tensor Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12393)
*Qun Dai,Liangjian Wen,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Dongkai Wang,Maolin Wang,Mingjie Wang,Jianzhuang Liu,He Yan,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HRIL，通过构建模态嵌入的跨矩张量并经Tucker分解获取核心张量，配合协同感知正则项，显式建模多模态高阶统计依赖，以保留协同信息容量，在协同任务与真实基准上均优于现有多模态对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督多模态表示学习虽取得进展，但协同信息（synergy）难以捕捉：此类任务相关信号仅由多模态联合构型产生，无法从任何单一模态中恢复。现有方法多关注跨模态共享信息，缺乏对联合交互的显式建模机制，导致协同信号容量在表示中被压缩。

Method: 核心观察是协同信息体现为模态间的高阶统计依赖。据此提出HRIL：在模态嵌入上构建经验跨矩张量（cross-moment tensor）表示多路交互；采用Tucker分解得到核心张量；并引入协同感知正则项（synergy-aware regularizer），防止能量集中，维持高阶耦合容量以捕获协同信息。

Result: 在受控协同任务（controlled synergy task）与真实世界基准上，HRIL相较现有多模态对比方法取得一致性提升，在协同交互主导的任务上增益尤为显著。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将协同信息刻画为高阶统计依赖并显式建模联合交互，HRIL为多模态表示中保留协同信号容量提供了有效途径，验证了高阶耦合建模在协同主导场景下的优势。

Abstract: Self-supervised multimodal representation learning has achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet capturing synergistic information remains challenging due to the complexity of cross-modal interactions. Unlike the shared information across individual modalities, synergy arises when task-relevant signals emerge only from the joint configuration of multiple modalities and cannot be recovered from any modality in isolation. This work focuses on how to preserve the information capacity for such synergistic signals in multimodal representations. The key observation is that synergistic information is reflected in higher-order statistical dependence among modalities, which provides a principled target for explicitly modeling joint interactions. Motivated by this insight, we propose Higher-order Representation and Information Learning (HRIL), which constructs an empirical cross-moment tensor over modality embeddings to represent multi-way interactions. HRIL employs Tucker decomposition to obtain a core tensor, complemented by a synergy-aware regularizer that prevents energy concentration and preserves higher-order coupling capacity for synergistic information capture. Experiments on the controlled synergy task and real-world benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing multimodal contrastive methods, with notable gains on tasks dominated by synergistic interactions. Code is released at https://github.com/brightest66/HRIL.

</details>


### [211] [Searching for "Harmful Refusal": A Psychometric Audit of an AI Safety Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12409)
*Christopher M. Stewart,Preston Botter,Natalie Sarabosing,Muye Zhang,Rachel Phinnemore,Shalini Ghosh,Hong Shen,Hoda Heidari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文质疑HELM Safety中"有害拒答"能否作为单一可测属性，用构念效度框架检验后发现问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全基准通常只报告整套数据集的单一总分，导致总分相近的模型其属性轮廓可能差异极大；在单属性层面比较模型更可行，但尚不清楚单个数据集分数能否隔离某个属性。作者欲检验有害拒答（模型拒绝危险或违规提示的倾向）是否构成单一可测属性。

Method: 采用构念效度框架（属性须先存在，测试才能测量它）。先筛选HELM Safety中可能针对有害拒答的四个数据集，发现其中三个已饱和；对剩余的HarmBench施加两项心理测量检验：多维项目反应理论（MIRT）建模；以及差异项目功能（DIF）分析。

Result: MIRT建模强烈表明HarmBench并不测量单一属性；DIF分析发现，在拒答能力得分相同的条件下，来自不同开发方的模型在某些题目上得分不同。这些DIF标记在限定范围的匹配下大多消失，该模式与聚合效应一致，但不足以排除领域特异的开发方差异。

Conclusion: HarmBench将不同危害行为压缩为单一分数，HELM safety总分又将HarmBench与其他数据集分数进一步聚合为一个顶层数字。任何跨数据集与题目求平均的安全分数都可能掩盖饱和并混淆行为。分数在用于模型比较前，应当先证明其具备单一属性可解释性。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks typically report one overall score for a suite of datasets, each of which may target one or more safety-related attributes, so models with similar overall scores can have very different attribute profiles. Comparing models is more tractable at the level of individual attributes, yet it is often unclear whether even a single dataset's scores isolate any single attribute. One plausible candidate for such an attribute is harmful refusal, a model's tendency to refuse dangerous or policy-violating prompts. We examine whether it constitutes a single, measurable attribute in HELM Safety. Using a construct validity framework that stipulates that an attribute must exist before a test can measure it, we start with HELM Safety's four datasets that might plausibly target harmful refusal, but find that three are saturated. We subject the remaining dataset, HarmBench, to two psychometric tests to determine if a single attribute like harmful refusal could stand behind its score. First, multidimensional item response theory modeling strongly suggests that HarmBench does not measure a singular attribute. Second, a differential item functioning analysis finds items where models from different developers with the same refusal ability score differently. These flags largely disappear under scope-specific matching, a pattern consistent with aggregation effects but not sufficient to rule out domain-specific developer differences. Zooming out, HarmBench collapses distinct harm behaviors into one score, and the overall HELM safety aggregate further collapses HarmBench and scores from other datasets into a single top-line number. Any safety score that averages over datasets and items can hide saturation and conflate behaviors this way. We argue that a score should earn its single-attribute reading before models are compared with it.

</details>


### [212] [BrickBench: Evaluating Agentic Brick Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12452)
*Peter Kulits,Yiqing Xu,R. Kenny Jones,Cordelia Schmid,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BrickBench 基准与 BrickAgent 环境，用于评估智能体依据文本提示设计可实体搭建的乐高模型，从有效性、语义对齐与设计质量三方面打分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多关注文本或代码任务，缺乏对"文本条件驱动的实体可搭建设计"这一兼具离散部件选择与局部/全局约束联合推理能力的评测。

Method: 构建 BrickBench 基准：给定提示，智能体从离散零件库中选件并生成装配方案；在规模与零件可用性不同的三种设定下评测；提供 BrickAgent 环境，供编码智能体构建、检查与验证设计；从有效性（可物理搭建）、语义对齐、设计质量三方面评分。

Result: 主流智能体基本能满足可验证的物理与语义要求，但设计水平仍不及人类设计。

Conclusion: 智能体在可搭建性与语义合规上表现良好，但在更高层次的设计质量上存在明显差距；基准与环境已公开。

Abstract: We propose BrickBench, a benchmark for agentic text-conditioned LEGO-set design. Given a prompt, an agent is tasked with producing an assembly that not only satisfies semantic and design criteria, but that can also be physically built. To do so, it must select parts from a discrete library and reason jointly about local and global constraints. We score validity, alignment, and design across three settings that vary in scale and part availability. We provide BrickAgent, an environment for coding agents to construct, inspect, and validate their designs. We find that leading agents largely satisfy verifiable physical and semantic requirements, but fall short of human designs. We release our benchmark and environment at http://www.brickben.ch

</details>


### [213] [On the estimation and validity of AI time horizons---a statistical look at the METR plot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12466)
*Drew T. Nguyen,William Fithian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用样条与项目反应理论重算METR的50%时间跨度，放松"任务AI难度线性依赖人类时间对数"的假设，得到更优的时间跨度点估计与诊断图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: METR的50%时间跨度以可解释的人类完成时间单位表达AI能力，但原始计算假设任务的AI难度与人类时间的对数呈线性关系，该假设可能不成立，需检验并放松。

Method: 在228个任务、26个AI上，采用样条和项目反应理论（IRT）重新计算时间跨度，拟合的样条可解释为将人类时间换算为AI难度的函数；并用交叉验证下的恰当评分规则（proper scoring rules）与诊断图评估。

Result: 拟合样条在2–30分钟区间近乎平坦，其余区间接近线性；因此时间跨度从3分钟跳到30分钟远比从30分钟跳到5小时容易，尽管两者倍数同为10×。新点估计在交叉验证评分规则下表现更好。

Conclusion: 建议时间跨度应结合诊断图解释，尤其在新时间跨度基准被提出或已有基准扩展到更长任务时，以评估其构念效度。

Abstract: METR's 50\% time horizon measures the human completion time of software tasks that an AI solves with 50\% probability, allowing AI capabilities to be expressed in interpretable units. On 228 tasks and 26 AIs, we recompute the time horizons using splines and item-response theory to relax the assumption that the AI difficulty of a task depends linearly on the log of human time. Our fitted spline can be interpreted as a function that \emph{converts} human time to AI difficulty; it is nearly flat in a region from 2--30 min but close to linear elsewhere. Hence, a time-horizon jump from 3 min to 30 min is much easier than one from 30 min to 5 hours despite the same multiplier of $10 \times$. Overall, we contribute time-horizon point estimates that perform better under a cross-validated suite of proper scoring rules, as well as diagnostic plots for assessing time horizons' construct validity. We suggest that time horizons be interpreted together with the diagnostic plots, especially as new time-horizon-based benchmarks are proposed or existing ones grow to include longer tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Decentralized collaborative continual learning: A multi-objective minimization-based technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10882)
*Yara Zgheib,Marc Antonini,Roula Nassif*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将去中心化持续学习建模为多目标优化问题：智能体在通信图上仅做局部计算并与邻居交换信息，顺序到达的任务带来稳定性-可塑性困境。智能体在本地记忆缓冲区存储旧任务样本，通过多目标形式将记忆信息纳入学习，使参数更新兼顾当前与历史任务。在均方误差意义下分析了该方法的性能，证明智能体间的协作可提升持续学习性能，并缓解遗忘、改善跨任务的平均MSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化（分布式流式）场景中任务随时间顺序到达，智能体既要适应新任务又需保留旧知识，从而引发稳定性-可塑性困境；同时，现有持续学习多集中式处理，缺少在通信图约束下、且能兼顾稳定性的去中心化协同学习与理论分析。

Method: 1）将去中心化持续学习纳入多目标优化框架，任务t对应所有智能体代价函数的共同极小点；2）智能体仅进行局部计算，并通过通信图与邻居交换信息；3）为应对稳定性，智能体在本地记忆缓冲区存储过去任务的样本子集；4）通过适当的多目标建模，将存储信息融入学习过程，使参数更新同时考虑当前任务与已学任务。

Result: 在关于个体代价函数与梯度噪声过程的一般假设下，从均方误差角度进行了理论分析，结果表明智能体间的合作能提升持续学习性能：通过与邻居交换信息，去中心化协同学习可利用局部观测数据与记忆缓冲区的多样性，改善跨任务的网络平均均方偏差（MSD）。仿真验证了理论结论，并显示该方法能减少遗忘、提升跨任务平均MSD。

Conclusion: 去中心化持续学习可通过多目标优化与本地记忆缓冲区的结合，在通信图约束下有效平衡稳定性与可塑性；智能体协作能利用数据与记忆的多样性提升跨任务平均MSD，理论与仿真均表明该方法的有效性和降低遗忘的能力。

Abstract: In this work, we formulate decentralized continual learning within a multi-objective optimization framework. For a given inference task t (corresponding to a common minimizer shared by the cost functions of all agents), agents collecting data in a distributed and streamed manner are only allowed to perform local computations and to exchange information with neighboring agents over the underlying communication graph. As tasks evolve sequentially over time, agents must adapt to newly arriving tasks while retaining knowledge acquired from previously learned ones. This requirement leads to the wellknown stability plasticity dilemma, where stability refers to the ability to retain previous knowledge, while plasticity refers to the ability to learn and adapt to new tasks. To address the stability challenge, agents store subsets of samples from past tasks in local memory buffers. Then, through an appropriate multiobjective formulation, the stored information is incorporated into the learning process so that parameter updates account jointly for the current task and previously learned tasks. The proposed decentralized continual learning approach is analyzed in the mean square error sense under general assumptions on the individual cost functions and gradient noise processes. The analysis reveals that cooperation among agents improves the performance of continual learning. In particular, by exchanging information with neighboring agents, decentralized collaborative learning can exploit the diversity of locally observed data and memory buffers to improve the network average mean-square deviation (MSD) across tasks. Finally, simulations illustrate the theoretical findings and the effectiveness of the method in reducing forgetting and improving the average MSD across tasks.

</details>


### [215] [Personalization Matters: Long-Horizon Conversation Agent with User-Centric Information in Online Shopping Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11375)
*Rena Gao,Yue Dai,Hao Guan,Shengxiang Gao,Wangyang Wu,Yixin Shen,Jey Han Lau*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出多智能体、多模态RAG框架，通过角色分解与用户中心检索提升多轮个性化购物对话的一致性与个性化感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化对话式购物需在多轮交互中维持偏好一致性，用户会逐步透露约束条件；现有方法依赖静态用户画像，且未显式控制长程交互行为。

Method: 构建多智能体、多模态RAG框架，将对话状态跟踪、推荐检索、偏好感知推理与回复生成解耦；融合商品元数据、商品评论、图像生成描述及用户历史评论；采用轨迹级评测协议，含全局偏好一致性、累积信息综合、交互轨迹与语气一致性四个维度。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews 2023基准上，启用检索的变体在自动轨迹指标上优于无RAG基线（平均4.82 vs. 3.74）；小规模真实用户研究（n=5）中，Full变体取得最高平均总体评分（4.60 vs. 基线2.20）。

Conclusion: 角色分解结合用户中心检索可提升感知个性化，为多轮对话式购物提供初步证据；但用户研究样本量小，结论具探索性。

Abstract: Personalized conversational shopping requires maintaining preference consistency over multi-turn interactions, where users reveal constraints gradually. Existing approaches often rely on static profiles and do not explicitly control long-horizon interaction behavior. We propose a multi-agent, multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework that decomposes dialogue state tracking, recommendation retrieval, preference-aware reasoning, and response generation, while integrating product metadata, product reviews, image-derived descriptions, and user historical reviews. To evaluate interaction-level quality, we adopt a trajectory-level protocol with four dimensions: Global Preference Consistency, Cumulative Information Synthesis, Interaction Trajectory, and Tone Consistency. On an Amazon Reviews 2023 benchmark, retrieval-enabled variants outperform a no-RAG baseline on automatic trajectory metrics (average 4.82 vs. 3.74). In a small real-user study ($n{=}5$), the Full variant achieves the highest mean overall rating (4.60 vs. 2.20 for Baseline), providing exploratory evidence that role decomposition plus user-centric retrieval improves perceived personalization.\footnote{Code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/RenaGao/Multimodel_RAG_Indexing

</details>


### [216] [ConventionPlay: Capability-Limited Training for Robust Ad-Hoc Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11842)
*Abhishek Sriraman,Eleni Vasilaki,Robert Loftin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ConventionPlay，通过在与不同适应度伙伴种群对抗训练中学习发现伙伴最优约定，在多人约定兼容的测试种群上超越现有临时协作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临时协作强化学习假设伙伴只遵循单一固定约定，忽视了部分伙伴可适应多个约定的可能性，导致智能体无法主动探测并引导伙伴达成更优联合策略。

Method: 构建包含不同适应度伙伴的训练种群：部分伙伴遵循单一固定约定，部分伙伴可适应任务可能约定的一个子集；智能体与该种群对抗训练，学习主动探测伙伴能力并将其引导至其可执行的最有效联合策略。

Result: 在由兼容多种约定的伙伴构成的测试种群上，ConventionPlay训练的智能体性能优于现有临时协作方法。

Conclusion: 考虑伙伴约定适应度的多样性能够促使智能体主动探测并引导伙伴，从而在临时协作中取得更优的联合策略与性能。

Abstract: Ad-hoc collaboration often requires agents to identify and adhere to some shared convention within a cooperative task. Existing work on reinforcement learning (RL) for ad-hoc collaboration focuses on training agents that adapt to the conventions established by their partners. These methods fail to consider the possibility that while some partners might follow only a single fixed convention, others may themselves be capable of adapting to multiple conventions. Here we present ConventionPlay, an RL-based approach that teaches agents to discover their partner's optimal convention by training against a learned population of partners that exhibit different degrees of adaptability across conventions. Some of these partners follow a single, fixed convention, while others are able to adapt to a subset of the possible conventions for the task in question. The existence of partners that support a limited subset of conventions forces agents trained against this population to actively probe their partner's capabilities, and steer their partner towards the most effective joint strategy that they are capable of following. Our experimental results demonstrate that agents trained via ConventionPlay achieve superior performance to existing ad-hoc collaboration methods against test populations of partners that are compatible with multiple conventions.

</details>


### [217] [Spatial Pattern Formation from Multi-Agent Learning in Public Goods Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12321)
*Yefei Zhang,Yuxuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文用表格型Q学习研究合作者与叛逃者在空间公共品博弈中自主学习移动策略的过程，发现学习率组合会显著影响聚集、移动带等空间模式的形成及集体福利，并且通过对拥挤外部性收费可挽回大部分福利损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有空间公共品模型通常假定向更富裕地点移动的规则是外生给定的，忽略了个体如何通过学习决定移动。作者希望回答两个问题：当智能体自主学习移动位置时，空间模式如何涌现；学习率又如何塑造这些模式对集体福利的影响。

Method: 构建包含固定数量合作者与叛逃者的空间公共品模型。两类智能体均使用表格型Q学习，仅依赖局部观测，独立学习移动策略。考察不同学习率组合以及不同训练预算下策略与空间模式的变化，并测试对个体造成的拥挤外部性收费这一干预手段的效果。

Result: 合作者学习会在资源峰值周围形成聚集，共同适应会改变聚集强度与移动方式。在固定训练预算下，福利损失最大出现在合作者学习率高、叛逃者学习率低的组合。该情形中学习到的策略还会形成由共享方向偏好支撑的移动带。随训练继续，支持移动带的条件会变化，说明这些模式反映的是训练历史而非稳定渐近结果。在所有测试的合作者学习条件下，平均集体福利低于随机移动，原因是拥挤加剧超过了资源收益。对拥挤外部性收费可在测试条件下挽回大部分福利损失。

Conclusion: 学习率是决定个体奖励驱动下空间组织形态及其福利成本的关键因素。学习产生的空间模式具有路径依赖和非渐近性质；拥挤外部性收费是缓解集体福利损失的有效机制。研究将学习动力学与空间公共品中的组织涌现和福利后果联系起来。

Abstract: Spatial public goods models show that prescribed movement toward richer locations can generate spatial patterns. We ask how such patterns emerge when agents learn where to move and how learning rates shape their consequences for collective welfare. Fixed populations of cooperators and defectors independently learn movement policies using tabular Q-learning and local observations. Cooperator learning generates clusters around resource peaks, while co-adaptation changes their strength and motion. At a fixed training budget, the largest welfare losses occur when cooperators learn at high rates and defectors at low rates. In part of this regime, learned policies also generate traveling bands supported by a shared directional preference. The conditions supporting travel change with further training, so these patterns reflect training history rather than an established asymptotic outcome. Across the tested learning-rate conditions with cooperator learning, mean collective welfare falls below random movement because increased crowding outweighs gains in resource benefit. Charging agents for the crowding they impose on others during learning recovers much of the welfare loss in the tested conditions. These results connect learning rates to the emergence and welfare costs of spatial organization driven by individual rewards.

</details>


### [218] [Mental-Models for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12453)
*Hanan Gani,Lulu Shao,Manmohan Chandraker*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出"心智模型赋能智能体"框架，通过联合学习摊销递归心智理论（ToM）表征与信念条件奖励模型，使智能体在不完全观测下推断伙伴的隐藏信念、意图与反应，并据此指导决策，在纯语言与多模态基准上均提升了交互质量与ToM表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体鲁棒决策需要在部分可观测条件下推理其他智能体所知、所图与所为。现有智能体系统多依赖提示设计、记忆或端到端行为塑造，通常未学习可跨任务复用、可作为决策变量的显式伙伴状态表征。

Method: 提出mental-model-enabled agents框架：从观测历史推断伙伴的隐藏信念、意图与可能反应。方法学习一种摊销的递归心智理论表征，包含一阶与二阶心智状态结构，并与信念条件奖励模型联合训练（基于推断的伙伴状态评估候选动作）；随后在该信念感知信号下学习策略，使智能体在推理时可独立行动，同时保留显式伙伴建模的收益。

Result: 在同一框架下于纯语言与多模态基准进行评测，相较于基线智能体系统，显式心智状态建模在两类设置中均持续提升交互质量与心智理论性能。

Conclusion: 结构化的伙伴建模是通用多智能体系统的有效归纳偏置。代码已公开于 https://github.com/hananshafi/Mental-Models。

Abstract: Large foundation models have accelerated progress toward general-purpose agents that interact with humans and other agents through language and multimodal signals. However, robust multi-agent decision-making requires reasoning about what other agents know, intend, and are likely to do under partial observability. Current agentic systems often operate through prompt design, memory, or end-to-end behavioral shaping, but typically do not learn an explicit partner-state representation that can be reused as a decision variable across tasks. We introduce \emph{mental-model-enabled agents}, a framework that equips an agent with a latent mental model of its counterpart, allowing it to infer hidden beliefs, intentions, and likely reactions from the observed history and use these inferences to guide action selection. Our method learns an amortized recursive Theory-of-Mind representation, with first- and second-order mental-state structure, jointly with a belief-conditioned reward model that evaluates candidate actions relative to the inferred partner state. A policy is then learned under this belief-aware signal, yielding an agent that can act independently at inference time while retaining the benefits of explicit partner modeling. We evaluate the same framework on both language-only and multimodal benchmarks. Across these settings, explicit mental-state modeling consistently improves interaction quality and Theory-of-Mind performance over base agentic systems, showing that structured partner modeling is a useful inductive bias for general multi-agent systems. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/hananshafi/Mental-Models

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [LIFT: A Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer for Unified Retrieval and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10556)
*Keji Miao,Enhao Cheng,Yan Li,Qun Li,Qi Zhang,Jie Yuan,Xiaoyong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 LIFT（Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer），将每次交互分解为 Request、Item、Context、Action 四个有序状态并建模为因果序列，使检索与排序共享历史建模同时保留各自阶段特有信息。在 ML-20M 与 Taobao 上取得最优 Joint Score，分别较最强基线提升 4.9% 与 3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联推荐系统中，检索与排序阶段使用相同的用户历史，但两个阶段在不同交互节点可获得的信息并不相同。这种"信息同源、需求异构"的矛盾导致历史建模难以兼顾两个阶段的差异化信息需求。

Method: 提出 Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer（LIFT）：将每次交互拆解为有序的 Request、Item、Context、Action 状态，并作为因果序列建模。检索阶段读取 Request 状态，排序阶段读取 Context 状态，从而在共享历史建模的同时保留阶段特定信息。具体以 Role-Conditioned Attention 与轻量 Pre-LN Bias 实现该表示。

Result: 在 ML-20M 与 Taobao 数据集上，LIFT 在参与评测的联合模型中取得最高 Joint Score，较最强基线分别提升 4.9% 和 3.6%。损失权重扫描显示其具有较好的检索—排序权衡；消融与扩展性分析进一步考察了生命周期序列构建、模型组件与容量设置。

Conclusion: LIFT 通过将交互建模为阶段感知的因果序列，缓解了级联推荐中检索与排序共享历史却信息需求不同的问题，在多个数据集上验证了有效性，并具备良好的检索—排序权衡与可扩展性。

Abstract: Cascaded recommender systems use the same user history for retrieval and ranking, while the two stages have access to different information at different points of an interaction. We propose the Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer (LIFT), which decomposes each interaction into ordered Request, Item, Context, and Action states and models them as a causal sequence. Retrieval reads the Request state, while ranking reads the Context state, allowing both tasks to share history modeling while preserving stage-specific information. LIFT instantiates this representation with Role-Conditioned Attention and a lightweight Pre-LN Bias. On ML-20M and Taobao, LIFT achieves the highest Joint Score among the evaluated joint models, improving over the strongest baselines by 4.9% and 3.6%, respectively. Loss-weight sweeps show favorable retrieval--ranking trade-offs, while ablations and scaling analyses further examine lifecycle sequence construction, model components, and capacity settings.

</details>


### [220] [Learning Multi-Step Query Rewriting via Corpus Feedback for Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10955)
*João Coelho,Hong Wang,Jie Yuan,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文将对话式查询改写(CQR)重新建模为序贯检索问题：智能体在包含三类改写操作与停止动作的类型化动作空间中迭代地改写并检索，利用检索质量奖励经SFT+RL训练，无需人工改写标注，在TopiOCQA、QReCC及CAsT上取得优于基线的检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流CQR方法在检索前一次性完成改写，改写结果在未获得语料证据的情况下即被固定，无法借助检索到的文档来纠正指代消解或词汇选择，限制了改写质量与最终检索性能。

Method: 将CQR重构为序贯决策问题：智能体在当前轮次执行改写→检索→基于返回段落条件化下一次改写的循环。动作空间为类型化的三种改写操作（将对话意图解析为独立查询、生成词汇重写、合成伪文档以进行文档到文档匹配）加一个终止动作。策略先经监督微调再以单一检索质量奖励做强化学习，全程不使用人工改写标注。

Result: 在TopiOCQA与QReCC上优于多个检索对齐基线；对不同检索后端保持有效性；无需额外训练即可泛化至CAsT基准。分析表明，仅通过检索奖励优化，学习到的策略自发形成将伪文档锚定于前序步骤检索所得段落的行为，显著提升检索效果。

Conclusion: 检索证据反馈可有效纠正改写，序贯化CQR配合纯检索奖励的RL训练能够在不依赖人工改写标注的前提下学到可解释且可迁移的改写行为，为对话检索提供了一种统一且可泛化的框架。

Abstract: Conversational Query Rewriting (CQR) turns a context dependent user turn into a standalone query for a retriever, and most methods do this in a single step from the dialogue history before retrieving once. The rewrite is therefore fixed before any corpus evidence is available to correct its reference resolution or its vocabulary. We recast CQR as a sequential retrieval problem: an agent rewrites the current turn, retrieves, and conditions its next rewrite on the returned passages. The agent acts in a typed space of three rewriting operations, resolving conversational intent into a standalone query, generating lexical reformulations, or synthesizing pseudo-documents for document-to-document matching, together with a stop action that ends the episode. We train the policy with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning against a single retrieval-quality reward, using no human rewrite annotations. Across TopiOCQA and QReCC, the agent outperforms several retrieval-aligned baselines, while remaining effective across retrieval backends and generalizing to the CAsT benchmarks without additional training. Further analysis shows that, through retrieval-reward optimization alone, the learned policy develops a behavior of grounding pseudo-documents in passages retrieved by earlier steps, substantially improving retrieval.

</details>


### [221] [H2CE: Modeling Geo-Semantic Interactions for POI Reranking with Heterogeneous Two-Stage Cross-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11277)
*Zhengwei Bai,Moreno D'Incà,Danielle Class,Alessandro Moschitti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: H2CE 是一种异构两阶段交叉编码器，用于延迟受限的 POI 重排序：通过分桶文本描述与精确标量双通道表示数值属性，潜在空间融合语义与数值嵌入，并采用“全候选逐点打分 + Top-K 两两比较（Copeland 聚合）”架构，将成对计算复杂度从 O(N²) 降至 O(N+K(K-1))。在 5,743 条查询测试集上达到 67.48% NDCG@5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地搜索中的 POI 重排序需要建模查询条件下词法语义、地理邻近性与数值质量信号（评分、评论数）之间的权衡：近的 POI 可能只部分满足查询意图，远的 POI 可能具备更强的语义与质量证据。同时该任务受实时服务延迟约束，现有方法难以兼顾细粒度相对权衡与计算可行性。

Method: 1）数值属性双通道表示：将分桶后的自然语言描述符插入交叉编码器输入以支持语义—数值注意力，精确标量值则由专用 MLP 处理以保留量级信息；2）语义嵌入与数值嵌入通过潜在空间聚合进行融合，实现超越标量加权和的非线性交互；3）两阶段架构：Stage 1 对所有候选项进行逐点打分以支持可扩展过滤，Stage 2 对 Top-K 候选进行两两（head-to-head）比较并采用 Copeland 聚合，使细粒度相对权衡显式化；4）配套对齐训练。

Result: 在 5,743 条查询的本地搜索测试集上，H2CE 取得 67.48% NDCG@5，较 XGBoost LTR 绝对提升 +22.82%，较零样本 LLM 重排序器提升 +35.89%；两两比较阶段在纯逐点模型基础上再带来 +1.98% NDCG@5。消融实验验证了数值特征、潜在聚合、Top-K 两两重排序与对齐训练的有效性。

Conclusion: H2CE 通过数值属性的异构双通道表示、潜在空间融合与两阶段（逐点过滤 + Top-K 成对比较）架构，在延迟受限条件下有效建模语义、地理与数值质量之间的查询相关权衡，显著优于 LTR 基线与零样本 LLM 重排序器，且各设计组件均获消融支持。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) reranking in local search must model query-conditioned tradeoffs among lexical semantics, geospatial proximity, and numerical quality signals such as rating and review count, while remaining practical under real-time serving constraints. A close POI may only partially satisfy the query intent, while a farther one may offer stronger semantic and quality evidence. We present H2CE, a Heterogeneous Two-stage Cross-Encoder for latency-bounded POI reranking. H2CE represents numerical attributes in two complementary ways: bucketized natural-language descriptors are inserted into the cross-encoder input to support semantic--numeric attention, while exact scalar values are processed by dedicated MLPs to preserve magnitude information. The resulting semantic and numerical embeddings are fused through latent-space aggregation, enabling nonlinear interactions beyond scalar weighted sums. H2CE then applies a two-stage architecture: Stage 1 scores all candidates pointwise for scalable filtering, and Stage 2 performs head-to-head pairwise comparison among the top-$K$ candidates with Copeland aggregation, making fine-grained relative tradeoffs explicit while reducing pairwise cost from O(N^2) to O(N+K(K-1)). On a 5,743-query local search test set, H2CE achieves 67.48% NDCG@5, improving over XGBoost LTR by +22.82% absolute and over a zero-shot LLM reranker by +35.89%. The pairwise stage adds +1.98% NDCG@5 over the pointwise model alone. Ablations confirm the value of numerical features, latent aggregation, top-K pairwise reranking, and aligned training.

</details>


### [222] [EVIE: Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11553)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Qian-Wen Zhang,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EVIE系列原生视觉文档检索器，通过证据引导数据治理、双向师生学习与层次聚类索引压缩，在精度与存储间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法难以兼顾细粒度页面理解与高效索引：基于OCR的文本检索存在预处理延迟且丢失视觉与结构信息；单向量视觉语言模型虽免OCR，但整页压缩为单向量限制了匹配粒度；多向量MaxSim检索交互更细，却需要大索引且精度仍有提升空间。作者认为需在表示学习与索引构建全过程中保留与查询相关的页面证据。

Method: EVIE包含三项创新：(1) 证据判定的数据治理，用多模态评判器识别含答案的正样本并过滤不可靠负样本；(2) 双向师生学习，采用对称listwise蒸馏与基于前缀的Matryoshka表示学习（Prefix-MRL），使单个学生检查点支持六种嵌套嵌入维度而无需重编码；(3) 层次凝聚索引压缩（HAC），对页面token进行空间正则化聚类并存储语义质心，实现单阶段MaxSim检索。

Result: 在ViDoRe V1、V2、V3及JinaVDR共138个任务上验证：EVIE-8B在V3上达66.75 nDCG@10，超过最佳外部基线1.43分，四套件平均79.51；EVIE-4.5B结合HAC在每百万页仅3.81 GiB下保持59.58 nDCG@10，向量负载减少128倍。

Conclusion: EVIE通过在整个表示学习与索引构建流程中保留查询相关页面证据，显著改善了视觉文档检索的精度—存储权衡。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable visual document retrieval (VDR) requires both fine-grained page understanding and efficient indexing, yet existing approaches struggle to achieve both. OCR-based text retrieval adds preprocessing latency and can lose visual and structural cues needed to understand complex pages. Single-vector vision-language models bypass OCR, but compressing an entire page into one vector limits the granularity of query--document matching. Multi-vector retrievers with MaxSim provide finer interactions, yet demand large indexes and still leave room for accuracy improvements. We argue that overcoming these limitations requires preserving query-relevant page evidence throughout representation learning and index construction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{\textit{EVIE}} (Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings), a family of native visual document retrievers integrating three key innovations: (1) Evidence-judged data governance, which uses a multimodal judge to identify answer-bearing positives and filter unreliable negatives. (2) Bidirectional teacher--student learning with symmetric listwise distillation and prefix-based Matryoshka representation learning (Prefix-MRL), enabling one student checkpoint to serve six nested embedding dimensions without re-encoding. (3) Hierarchical agglomerative index compression (HAC), which clusters page tokens with spatial regularization and stores semantic centroids for single-stage MaxSim retrieval. Extensive experiments across 138 tasks from ViDoRe V1, V2, V3, and JinaVDR validate EVIE. EVIE-8B achieves 66.75 nDCG@10 on V3, exceeding the best external baseline by 1.43 points, with a four-suite average of 79.51. EVIE-4.5B with HAC retains 59.58 nDCG@10 at only 3.81 GiB per million pages, reducing vector payload by $128\times$. Together, these results improve the accuracy--storage trade-off for visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [223] [Overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11598)
*Junjie Chen,Yuxi Dong,Haitao Li,Yiqun Liu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文概述了NTCIR-19的AEOLLM-2任务，在NTCIR-18 AEOLLM基础上新增"深度研究评估"子任务，聚焦LLM生成长文本报告的自动评价，共收到10支团队91个提交，并与人工标注进行比对评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在长文本生成场景（如深度研究报告）中的广泛应用，如何自动、可靠地评估其生成质量成为亟待解决的问题。基于NTCIR-18 AEOLLM的成功经验，需要进一步探索面向长文本生成的自动评价方法。

Method: 在NTCIR-19提出AEOLLM-2任务，新增"深度研究评估"（Deep Research Evaluation）子任务。参赛者开发自动评价方法，对LLM生成的长篇深度研究报告质量进行打分；通过将方法得分与人工标注的真实标签（ground-truth）进行比对来衡量各方法性能。文中还介绍了任务背景、数据集构建、评价指标及参赛方法。

Result: 本届共收到来自10支团队的91个提交（runs）。各参赛方法的性能通过与人工标注标签的比对结果进行衡量，并给出了最终评测结果。

Conclusion: AEOLLM-2通过引入深度研究评估子任务，推动了LLM长文本生成自动评价的研究，为面向长文本的自动评估方法提供了基准与实验平台。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide an overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) task. Building on the success of the NTCIR-18 core task AEOLLM, we proposed AEOLLM-2 for NTCIR-19 to further investigate automatic evaluation methods for Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in long-form text generation scenarios. In AEOLLM-2, we introduced a new subtask, Deep Research Evaluation, which focuses on the automatic evaluation of long-form deep research reports generated by LLMs. Participants developed evaluation methods to automatically assess the quality of these reports, and the performance of each method was measured by comparing its scores against human-annotated ground-truth labels. This year, we received 91 runs from 10 teams in total. This paper describes the background of the task, the dataset construction, the evaluation measures, the participants' methods, and the final evaluation results.

</details>


### [224] [SkillContrast: Difference-Guided Text Selection for Agent Skill Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11650)
*Jiandong Ding,Honglei Ji,Ming Liu,Tao Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SkillContrast 是一种免训练的技能选择器，通过对比检索到的相似技能并保留其差异文本及局部上下文，为预训练重排器提供更紧凑且信息量更高的输入。在 SameCapRisk-Bench 上，其干净命中数比 TF-IDF 查询选择高 54-72 个，同时减少 51.1-58.8% 的模型输入 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似智能体技能可能共享指令，但在使用条件上存在差异。基于查询的文本选择容易保留共享指令，却忽略这些条件差异，导致检索到有帮助技能的同时也可能引入其风险性"兄弟"技能。

Method: 提出 SkillContrast：一种免训练的文本选择器。它对比检索到的多个技能，提取其差异文本并附带局部上下文，作为预训练重排器的输入。通过长度匹配的组件替换实验，识别差异文本与上下文各自的贡献。

Result: 在 SameCapRisk-Bench 的 1,235 条请求上，相同每候选输入长度下，SkillContrast 比 TF-IDF 查询选择多产生 54-72 个干净命中（跨 2 种检索器和 2 种重排器规模）。差异文本是主要贡献因素，上下文效应较小且混合。相较完整技能正文，模型输入 token 减少 51.1-58.8%；在 0.6B 模型上干净命中少 10-18 个，在 4B 模型上达到持平或更高。

Conclusion: 候选间的相对差异可以补充查询相关性，用于选择紧凑的重排输入；SkillContrast 在压缩输入的同时保持了竞争力甚至更优的重排效果。

Abstract: Similar agent skills can share instructions but differ in their conditions of use. Query-based text selection may retain shared instructions and omit these distinctions. We introduce SkillContrast, a training-free selector that compares retrieved skills and retains their differing text with local context for a pretrained reranker. On 1,235 requests from SameCapRisk-Bench, it yields 54-72 more clean hits (requests that retrieve a helpful skill without its marked risky sibling) than TF-IDF query selection at identical per-candidate input lengths, across 2 retrievers and 2 reranker sizes. Length-matched component replacements identify differing text as the main contributor in the primary setting, with smaller, mixed context effects. Relative to full skill bodies, SkillContrast uses 51.1-58.8% fewer model-input tokens, with 10-18 fewer clean hits at 0.6B and matching or higher observed clean-hit counts at 4B. Candidate-relative differences thus complement query relevance in selecting compact reranking inputs.

</details>


### [225] [Autoregressive Retriever: Improving Query Understanding from Item Feedback for Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11666)
*Jianfei Zhao,Yifan Wang,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Zhixing Tan,Yang Luo,Boyuan Pan,Xu Kai,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR)，一种多模态检索模型，通过迭代检索并利用反馈项内容更新查询表示来改进检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态检索通常只对查询编码一次，然后按嵌入相似度独立排序已索引项。这种方式虽高效，但即便检索到的项有助于澄清信息需求，查询表示也保持不变，无法利用交互反馈来改进搜索。

Method: ARR 在检索项与更新查询嵌入之间交替进行，最终用最终嵌入对集合排序。使用分步对比监督的监督微调让编码器学会利用反馈；用强化学习将反馈项视为动作，以相关项的最终倒数排名优化其选择；并通过查询侧适配器在固定项索引下实现该优化。

Result: ARR 在域内和零样本基准上均表现出强检索性能，平均优于所比较的基线。进一步分析表明，反馈在推理时能提升检索效果，且带反馈的训练在任何项被观察到之前也能改善初始查询嵌入。

Conclusion: 反馈驱动的自回归检索能有效改进多模态检索，既提升推理阶段的检索表现，也优化初始查询表示，为交互式检索提供新思路。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval typically encodes a query once and ranks independently indexed items by embedding similarity. This design supports efficient search, but leaves the query representation unchanged even when retrieved items could help clarify the information need. We introduce the AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR), a multimodal retrieval model that learns both to select informative items and to use their content to refine subsequent retrieval. ARR alternates between retrieving an item and updating the query embedding, then uses the final embedding to rank the collection. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the encoder to use feedback through stepwise contrastive supervision. Reinforcement learning treats feedback items as actions and optimizes their selection using the final reciprocal rank of a relevant item. A query-side adapter enables this optimization against a fixed item index. ARR demonstrates strong retrieval performance on both in-domain and zero-shot benchmarks, outperforming the compared baselines on average. Further analyses show that feedback improves retrieval at inference time and that training with feedback also improves the initial query embedding, before any item is observed.

</details>


### [226] [Chaos in the Text: Revealing the Modality Preference in Mixed-Modality Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11816)
*Yubo Sun,Chunyi Peng,Yukun Yan,Zhenghao Liu,Zhipeng Xu,Sen Mei,Linlin Xin,Zheni Zeng,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了混合模态（文本、图像、图文融合）语料下的稠密检索，发现性能对模态构成高度敏感并呈V型曲线，提出"文本混沌"与模态偏好现象，并给出Trident方法缓解该偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密检索在纯文本与纯图像语料上进展显著，但其能力能否可靠迁移到同时包含文本、图像及图文融合文档的混合语料仍不明确，作者希望揭示并解决这一盲区。

Method: 跨架构系统评估检索器对模态构成的敏感性；提出Trident，为每篇文档构建文本、图像、图文融合三种等价正样本视图，并通过Multi-Positive View InfoNCE联合优化相关性判别与正视图平衡。

Result: 检索性能随图像文档被语义对应文本替换而呈现显著V型曲线：单模态语料表现强，多模态共存时明显下降；无关文本比等量无关图像造成更严重退化（"文本混沌"）；原因在于模态偏好——文本表示获得系统性更高的相似度分数，使无关文本可超越相关图像。Trident在视觉文档与自然图像基准上，于CLIP与VLM架构均提升混合模态检索，降低对模态构成与文本干扰的敏感性，并提升平均单模态检索性能。

Conclusion: 混合模态检索的核心瓶颈是模态偏好导致的文本偏见；通过多视图等价正样本与平衡优化可有效缓解该偏差，提升混合与单模态场景下的检索鲁棒性与性能。

Abstract: Dense retrievers have made significant progress on text and image corpora, but whether these capabilities extend reliably to mixed corpora containing text, image, and fused text-image documents remains unclear. In this paper, we systematically examine retrievers across architectures and find that their performance is highly sensitive to modality composition. As image documents are progressively replaced with semantically corresponding text representations, retrieval performance follows a pronounced V-shaped curve, remaining strong on single-modality corpora but degrading substantially when modalities coexist. In particular, irrelevant text causes more severe degradation than an equal number of irrelevant images, a phenomenon we term Chaos in the Text. Further analysis reveals modality preference, whereby text representations receive systematically higher similarity scores, allowing irrelevant text to outrank relevant images. To mitigate this bias, we introduce Trident, which constructs text, image, and fused text-image views of each document as co-equal positives and jointly optimizes relevance discrimination and positive-view balance through Multi-Positive View InfoNCE. Experiments across visual document and natural image benchmarks show that trident improves mixed-modality retrieval on both CLIP-based and VLM-based architectures, reduces sensitivity to modality composition and text distractors, and increases average single-modality retrieval performance.

</details>


### [227] [Project Greenhouse: Progress Toward Fully Open and Sovereign Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11922)
*Jimmy Lin,Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Nour Jedidi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出"Project Greenhouse"项目，验证仅用少量GPU即可从头构建完全开放、主权可控的智能体检索模型；首个里程碑是用两阶段配方（从零预训练+监督微调）构建有竞争力的pointwise decoder-only重排器，并开源Gaggle模型族检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流做法依赖第三方开放权重骨干模型，导致训练过程无法端到端自主掌控。作者希望验证：仅借助常见公开数据集和有限算力，也能构建完全开放、主权独立的智能体检索模型。

Method: 两阶段训练配方：首先从零开始预训练（pre-training from scratch），随后进行监督微调（SFT）。不依赖任何第三方开放权重骨干，仅使用常见公开数据集，整项实验在少量GPU上完成。

Result: 成功构建了一个有竞争力的pointwise decoder-only重排器，且大部分实验仅需少量GPU即可完成，验证了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 证明了以有限算力从零构建开放主权检索模型的可行性，并开源数据、代码、配置及Gaggle模型族检查点，支持透明、独立的完整复现，为验证更宏观的论点迈出第一步。

Abstract: Project Greenhouse represents our exploration of a simple thesis: We believe that it is possible to build fully open and sovereign models for agentic search with only modest computational resources. As a first milestone, we describe how to build a competitive pointwise decoder-only reranker using a simple two-step recipe comprising pre-training from scratch followed by supervised fine-tuning, starting only from commonly available datasets. Contrary to the dominant approach in the literature, we do not rely on existing open-weight backbones from third parties, and thus we are fully in control of model training, from end to end. We were able to accomplish the bulk of our experiments using no more than a handful of GPUs. This report articulates the importance and benefits of our approach, and we share artifacts that enable transparent, independent reproduction of all aspects of model training. Beyond data, code, and configurations that capture our efforts, we also release checkpoints for our family of Gaggle models, demonstrating the feasibility of our approach and providing a first step toward validating our broader thesis.

</details>


### [228] [NativeScope: Relation-Localized Retrieval over Native Topology with a Correct Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12243)
*Long Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 NativeScope 的"先定界后排序"检索方法，利用数据系统已有的原生结构（章节归属、会话边界、原生顺序）将查询表示为 q -> (A, r, B)，先由锚点 A 与关系 r 选出原生单元范围，再在范围内用目标词 B 排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索通常仅依据文本块与问题的语义相似度进行排序，忽略了数据系统中已存在的结构信息（如章节归属、会话边界与原生顺序），导致检索范围过大、召回受限。

Method: NativeScope 将查询表示为 q -> (A, r, B)：锚点 A 与关系 r 通过 belonging、before、after 等算子选出原生单元，目标词 B 仅对与所选范围重叠的文本块排序；并提出内部变体 NS-FullQ，用完整问题对同一候选集排序。在 QASPER 与 LongMemEval 衍生的 200 条受控文档与记忆记录上，以 1,024 token 预算进行评估。

Result: NativeScope 的原生单元召回率为文档 89.28%、记忆 72.50%，较实例级 Dense RAG 分别提升 42.75 与 22.00 个百分点。NS-FullQ 达到 87.78% 与 68.50%，其与 NativeScope 的差异不具结论性，表明主要增益来自关系定界而非更短的排序查询。使用自动 Top-1 锚点时，记忆召回降至 35.50%。

Conclusion: 当锚点坐标与原生关系可靠时，NativeScope 效果显著；但硬定界机制会继承定位接口的错误，即错误锚点会直接传导至检索结果。

Abstract: Dense retrieval usually ranks text chunks by their semantic similarity to a question. This ignores structure that many data systems already store, including section membership, session boundaries, and native order. We propose NativeScope, a scope-then-rank method for queries with a known anchor and relation. It represents a query as q -> (A, r, B). The anchor A and relation r select native units through belonging, before, or after operators, and the target term B ranks only chunks that overlap the selected scope. An internal variant, NS-FullQ, ranks the same candidates with the full question. We evaluate both methods on 200 controlled document and memory records derived from QASPER and LongMemEval under a 1,024-token budget. NativeScope attains native-unit recall of 89.28 percent for documents and 72.50 percent for memories, improving over instance-wide Dense RAG by 42.75 and 22.00 percentage points. NS-FullQ reaches 87.78 percent and 68.50 percent; its differences from NativeScope are inconclusive, locating the primary gain in relational scoping rather than the shorter ranking query. With automatic Top-1 anchors, memory recall falls to 35.50 percent. NativeScope is therefore effective when anchor coordinates and native relations are reliable, but hard scoping inherits errors from the localization interface.

</details>


### [229] [Compact and Efficient Indexes for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12300)
*Franco Maria Nardini,Luca Rizzo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出对学习式稀疏检索索引（基于 SEISMIC）的两级压缩方案：倒排索引用 medoid 替代逐块稀疏摘要，前向索引对分量与取值分别做重排+Δ-gap 位打包与 4-bit 码本量化，并引入 JUMPDOT 块状点积内核，在等精度下实现更快、更省内存的检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式稀疏检索索引（如 SEISMIC）虽检索高效，但内存占用较大。现有 SOTA 检索数据结构的内存开销成为瓶颈，需要在\*\*不牺牲检索效率\*\*的前提下大幅降低内存占用。

Method: 从 SEISMIC 的两个层级入手：(1) 倒排索引：用 medoid（选已有文档作为块代表）替代昂贵的稀疏向量块摘要，将每块元数据从稀疏向量压缩为单个文档 ID。(2) 前向索引：对分量与取值均压缩——重排词表使共现分量相邻，用 SIMD 友好的 DOTPACKING8 对 Δ-gap 编码，将解压与点积求值融合；取值采用针对分量分布拟合的紧凑 4-bit 码本量化。此外提出 JUMPDOT——面向少量非零项查询的块状点积内核。前向索引压缩独立于 SEISMIC，可接入任意基于前向索引打分的系统（文中集成到 KANNOLO）。

Result: 在 MS MARCO 上使用三种 SOTA 学习式稀疏编码器的综合评测：等精度下，本方案查询速度最高比最优竞品快 5.3x，内存少约 3x；在最受限内存场景下，仍快至 1.9x，内存少至 3.9x。

Conclusion: 通过倒排索引 medoid 化与前向索引的双重压缩（重排+Δ-gap 位打包+4-bit 量化）及 JUMPDOT 内核，显著改善了学习式稀疏检索的速度-空间权衡，且前向索引压缩具有通用可插拔性。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to substantially reduce the memory footprint of learned sparse retrieval indexes without sacrificing the efficiency of state-of-the-art retrieval data structures. Building on SEISMIC, we revisit both levels of its design: the inverted index used to select candidates and the forward index used to score them. For the inverted index, we replace costly per-block summaries with medoids, namely existing documents elected as block representatives, collapsing the per-block metadata from a sparse vector to a single document identifier. For the forward index, we compress both components and values. We reorder the vocabulary to place co-occurring components closer together and encode the resulting $Δ$-gaps with DOTPACKING8, a SIMD-friendly bit-packing scheme that fuses decompression with dot-product evaluation; values are quantized with compact per-component 4-bit codebooks fitted to each component's distribution. We further introduce JUMPDOT, a blocked dot-product kernel tailored for queries that contain only a few non-zero entries. Our forward-index compression is independent of SEISMIC and can be plugged into any system relying on forward-index-based scoring, as we demonstrate by integrating it into KANNOLO. A comprehensive evaluation on MS MARCO with three state-of-the-art learned sparse encoders shows that our solutions markedly improve the speed-space trade-off of learned sparse retrieval: at equal accuracy, our indexes answer queries up to 5.3x faster than the best competitor while using about 3x less memory, and in the most memory-constrained regime, they remain up to 1.9x faster while using up to 3.9x less memory.

</details>
